📚 本期文献进展 / Literature Brief

  • 本期禽流感病毒研究集中呈现以下进展。
  • 所有未接种疫苗的雪貂都出现了严重的疾病并死于感染或被人道安乐死。疫苗接种 与灭活的 疫苗 降低了病毒滴度并延缓了疾病进展,但不能防止致命的结果。用减毒活 疫苗 免疫的六只雪貂中有五只在挑战中幸存下来,并显示出对鼻粘膜病毒复制的更有效控制。
  • 结果显示,具有六个糖基化位点的进化枝 2.3.4.4b H5 HA 优先与全长(长柄)N1 NA 配对,而来自具有七个糖基化位点的早期进化枝的 H5 HA 蛋白优先与具有柄缺失(短柄)的 N1 NA 蛋白配对。
  • 在 15 μg 剂量下,LNP-pDNA 疫苗 显着减少了呼吸道分泌物中的病毒脱落,并防止了同源 LPAIV H5N3 病毒攻击后肺部可检测到的病毒感染。二价 疫苗 诱导针对 H2 和 H6 亚型的 HI 抗体应答。
  • 总共分析了 749 份配对血清样本,在 疫苗接种 之前在相当大比例的个体中检测到可测量的交叉反应 NA 抑制活性。疫苗接种 适度增加了 NA 抑制滴度,尤其是在 2009 年大流行之后。 [10.1128/jvi.00929-26]。
  • This reporting window highlights the following research developments.
  • All unvaccinated ferrets developed severe disease and succumbed to infection or were humanely euthanized.
  • The results showed that clade 2.3.4.4b H5 HA with six glycosylation sites preferentially pairs with the full-length (long-stalk) N1 NA, whereas H5 HA proteins from earlier clades with seven glycosylation sites preferentially pair with N1 NA proteins with a stalk deletion (short-stalk).
  • At the 15-μg dose, the LNP-pDNA vaccine significantly reduced viral shedding in respiratory secretions and prevented detectable viral infection in the lungs following challenge with the homologous LPAIV H5N3 virus.
  • A total of 749 paired serum samples were analyzed, with measurable cross-reactive NA-inhibiting activity detected in a substantial proportion of individuals before vaccination.

📰 本期新闻动态 / News Brief

  • 本期禽流感病毒新闻集中呈现以下动态。
  • 泛美卫生组织 (PAHO) 发布关于美洲地区 禽流感 A(H5N1) 的流行病学警报。 [news-026970cb042028bc]。
  • 测试了使用火鸡疱疹病毒(HVT)表达 H9N2 禽流感 病毒血凝素抗原的重组 疫苗。疫苗 靶向 HVT 基因组 的 UL45-46 基因间区域,与传统 US2 基因座相比,显示出卓越的免疫原性和保护功效。
  • 监测 现场证实了 高致病性禽流感 病毒 H5N1 亚型在南极洲常驻和迁徙 野生鸟类 人群中的活跃传播。该研究还建立了南极病毒序列与当代南美谱系之间的直接遗传联系,包括在南大西洋沿岸海洋野生动物中检测到的菌株。
  • 据 CIDRAP 报道,北美地区 H5N1 禽流感活动持续上升。 [news-5aa16e384232feaf]。
  • This reporting window highlights the following Avian influenza A viruses news developments.
  • Epidemiological Alert issued by the Pan American Health Organization (PAHO) regarding Avian Influenza A(H5N1) in the Americas Region. [news-026970cb042028bc].
  • A recombinant vaccine using the herpesvirus of turkeys (HVT) to express the hemagglutinin antigen of H9N2 avian influenza virus was tested.
  • Field surveillance confirmed the active circulation of highly pathogenic avian influenza virus subtype H5N1 within resident and migratory wild bird populations in Antarctica. [news-11d509e285ba3c82].
  • Uptick in H5N1 avian flu activity continues across North America, reported by CIDRAP. [news-5aa16e384232feaf].

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: Nature Communications  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-11-25  |  DOI: 10.1038/s41467-026-79529-y  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 17, 1, 11796
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

季节性疫苗对人源性牛H5N1流感病毒的保护作用

Protective effect of seasonal vaccines against human-derived bovine H5N1 influenza virus
作者： Ryuta Uraki, Kiyoko Iwatsuki‐Horimoto, Maki Kiso, Mutsumi Ito, Seiya Yamayoshi, Masaki Imai, Yoshihiro Kawaoka, Kiyoko Iwatsuki-Horimoto
摘要中文翻译自 2024 年初以来,高致病性禽流感 H5N1 病毒(进化枝 2.3.4.4b)在美国奶牛中引起 暴发,并感染包括人类在内的其他哺乳动物。在这里,我们评估了两种市售季节性流感 疫苗 对人源性牛 H5N1 分离株 A/Texas/37/2024 对从未接触过流感的雌性雪貂的保护功效。所有未接种疫苗的雪貂都会患上严重疾病并死于感染或符合人道安乐死标准。使用灭活流感 疫苗 进行免疫可降低多个呼吸道和肺外组织中的病毒滴度并延缓疾病进展,但不能防止致命结果。相比之下,用活的减毒流感病毒 疫苗 免疫的六只雪貂中有五只在挑战中存活下来,并且对鼻粘膜中的病毒复制表现出更有效的控制。减毒活疫苗接种诱导更强的T细胞反应,而针对攻击病毒缺乏可检测到的中和活性表明细胞免疫而不是中和抗体可能有助于保护。这些发现表明,季节性减毒活流感 疫苗接种 可以通过呼吸道中的细胞 免疫应答 与抗原独特的牛源 H5N1 病毒提供交叉保护,防止致命感染。在这项研究中,作者表明,季节性减毒流感 疫苗接种 可能通过呼吸道细胞 免疫应答 与雪貂中抗原独特的牛源性 H5N1 病毒提供交叉保护,防止致命感染。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
鉴于美国奶牛中 高致病性禽流感 H5N1 的 暴发 及其感染包括人类在内的其他哺乳动物的能力,该研究旨在评估季节性流感 疫苗 对人源性牛 H5N1 流感病毒的保护作用。
设计与对象
该研究评估了两种季节性流感 疫苗 对接受牛源 H5N1 分离株 A/Texas/37/2024 攻击的未接触过流感的雌性雪貂的保护功效。该研究于 2026 年进行,涉及以雪貂为 宿主 模型的受控实验设置。
核心方法
研究人员使用灭活流感 疫苗 和活减毒流感 疫苗 对雪貂进行免疫,然后用牛源性 H5N1 病毒攻击它们。他们测量了呼吸道和肺外组织中的病毒滴度并评估了疾病进展。
主要结果
所有未接种疫苗的雪貂都出现了严重的疾病并死于感染或被人道安乐死。疫苗接种 与灭活的 疫苗 降低了病毒滴度并延缓了疾病进展,但不能防止致命的结果。用减毒活 疫苗 免疫的六只雪貂中有五只在挑战中幸存下来,并显示出对鼻粘膜病毒复制的更有效控制。
解释与创新
作者解释说,减毒活体 疫苗接种 会诱导更强的 T 细胞反应,这可能有助于抵御牛源性 H5N1 病毒,大多数免疫雪貂的存活和病毒复制减少就证明了这一点。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,季节性减毒活流感 疫苗 可能针对抗原不同的 H5N1 病毒提供交叉保护,这可以为 疫苗 开发策略和针对人畜共患流感威胁的公共卫生准备提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于动物模型,不包括人类数据,这可能会影响结果的普遍性。证据是强有力的,因为它直接比较了 疫苗 在受控环境中的疗效和明确的结果。

Protective effect of seasonal vaccines against human-derived bovine H5N1 influenza virus

Authors: Ryuta Uraki, Kiyoko Iwatsuki‐Horimoto, Maki Kiso, Mutsumi Ito, Seiya Yamayoshi, Masaki Imai, Yoshihiro Kawaoka, Kiyoko Iwatsuki-Horimoto
Original abstract (English)
Since early 2024, highly pathogenic avian influenza H5N1 virus (clade 2.3.4.4b) has caused outbreaks in U.S. dairy cattle, infecting other mammals including humans. Here, we evaluate the protective efficacy of two commercially available seasonal influenza vaccines against a human-derived bovine H5N1 isolate, A/Texas/37/2024, in influenza-naïve female ferrets. All unvaccinated ferrets develop severe disease and succumb to infection or meet humane euthanasia criteria. Immunisation with an inactivated influenza vaccine reduces viral titres in multiple respiratory and extrapulmonary tissues and delays disease progression but does not prevent fatal outcomes. By contrast, five of six ferrets immunised with a live attenuated influenza vaccine survive challenge and show more effective control of viral replication in the nasal mucosa. Live attenuated vaccination induces stronger T-cell responses, while the absence of detectable neutralising activity against the challenge virus suggests that cellular immunity, rather than neutralising antibodies, may contribute to protection. These findings demonstrate that seasonal live attenuated influenza vaccination can provide cross-protection against lethal infection with an antigenically distinct bovine-derived H5N1 virus, potentially through cellular immune responses in the respiratory tract. In this study, the authors show that seasonal live-attenuated influenza vaccination provides cross-protection against lethal infection with an antigenically distinct bovine-derived H5N1 virus in ferrets, potentially through respiratory cellular immune responses.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to assess the protective effect of seasonal influenza vaccines against a human-derived bovine H5N1 influenza virus, given the outbreaks of highly pathogenic avian influenza H5N1 in U.S. dairy cattle and its ability to infect other mammals including humans.
Design and population
The study evaluated the protective efficacy of two seasonal influenza vaccines in influenza-naïve female ferrets challenged with the bovine-derived H5N1 isolate A/Texas/37/2024. The study was conducted in 2026 and involved a controlled experimental setting with ferrets as the host model.
Core methods
The researchers used an inactivated influenza vaccine and a live attenuated influenza vaccine to immunize ferrets, then challenged them with the bovine-derived H5N1 virus. They measured viral titres in respiratory and extrapulmonary tissues and assessed disease progression.
Main results
All unvaccinated ferrets developed severe disease and succumbed to infection or were humanely euthanized. Vaccination with an inactivated vaccine reduced viral titres and delayed disease progression but did not prevent fatal outcomes. Five of six ferrets immunized with a live attenuated vaccine survived the challenge and showed more effective control of viral replication in the nasal mucosa.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that live attenuated vaccination induces stronger T-cell responses, which may contribute to protection against the bovine-derived H5N1 virus, as evidenced by the survival of most immunized ferrets and reduced viral replication.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that seasonal live attenuated influenza vaccines may offer cross-protection against antigenically distinct H5N1 viruses, which could inform strategies for vaccine development and public health preparedness against zoonotic influenza threats.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to an animal model and does not include human data, which may affect the generalizability of the results. The evidence is strong as it directly compares vaccine efficacy in a controlled setting with clear outcomes.

2024-2026 年埃及牲畜中 禽流感 病毒暴露的血清学证据。

Serologic Evidence of Avian Influenza Virus Exposure in Livestock, Egypt, 2024-2026.
作者： Mokhtar Gomaa, Rabeh El-Shesheny, Ahmed El Taweel, Omnia Kutkat, Hager Emam, Basant Helal, Ayat Swelam, Mohamed GabAllah, Ahmed Kandeil, Pamela P. McKenzie
摘要中文翻译美国牛群感染甲型H5N1流感病毒跨物种溢出引发全球关注。我们调查了地方性 禽流感 病毒是否可能蔓延到埃及的家畜中。我们在 2.5% 的测试动物中检测到 H9N2 流感抗体,在 2.9%-4.5% 的测试动物中检测到 H5N1 抗体。其他 监测 将阐明 宿主 感染动态。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查地方性 禽流感 病毒是否可能蔓延到埃及的家畜中。这项研究的动机是全球对美国牛 A (H5N1) 流感病毒 跨物种溢出 的关注。
设计与对象
该研究于 2024 年至 2026 年间在埃及进行,重点关注家畜。它涉及测试动物样本以寻找 禽流感 病毒暴露的证据。
核心方法
研究人员使用血清学测试来检测牲畜中针对特定 禽流感 亚型的抗体。他们分析了结果以确定接触这些病毒的 患病率。
主要结果
2.5%的测试动物中检测到H9N2流感抗体,而2.9%-4.5%的样本中检测到H5N1抗体。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,禽流感 病毒可能在家畜中传播,突出表明需要进一步调查 宿主 感染的动态。
科研与公卫意义
结果强调了额外的 监测 对于更好地了解 禽流感 病毒在牲畜种群中的传播和影响的重要性,这可以为公共卫生策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
由于其观察性质且缺乏详细的统计分析,该研究提供的证据有限,导致置信度评估适中。

Serologic Evidence of Avian Influenza Virus Exposure in Livestock, Egypt, 2024-2026.

Authors: Mokhtar Gomaa, Rabeh El-Shesheny, Ahmed El Taweel, Omnia Kutkat, Hager Emam, Basant Helal, Ayat Swelam, Mohamed GabAllah, Ahmed Kandeil, Pamela P. McKenzie
Original abstract (English)
Spillover of influenza A(H5N1) virus to cattle in the United States sparked global concern. We investigated whether endemic avian influenza viruses are potentially spilling over into domestic livestock in Egypt. We detected influenza H9N2 antibodies in 2.5% and H5N1 antibodies in 2.9%-4.5% of tested animals. Additional surveillance will elucidate host infection dynamics.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate whether endemic avian influenza viruses are potentially spilling over into domestic livestock in Egypt. The research was motivated by global concern over the spillover of influenza A(H5N1) virus to cattle in the United States.
Design and population
The study was conducted in Egypt between 2024 and 2026, focusing on domestic livestock. It involved testing a sample of animals for evidence of avian influenza virus exposure.
Core methods
The researchers used serologic testing to detect antibodies against specific avian influenza subtypes in livestock. They analyzed the results to determine the prevalence of exposure to these viruses.
Main results
Influenza H9N2 antibodies were detected in 2.5% of tested animals, while H5N1 antibodies were found in 2.9%-4.5% of the sample.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that avian influenza viruses may be circulating in livestock, highlighting the need for further investigation into the dynamics of host infection.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results underscore the importance of additional surveillance to better understand the spread and impact of avian influenza viruses in livestock populations, which could inform public health strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study provides limited evidence due to its observational nature and the absence of detailed statistical analysis, resulting in a moderate confidence assessment.

Journal: Journal of Virology  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-11-23  |  DOI: 10.1128/jvi.00929-26  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, e0092926
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

基于神经氨酸酶的人类 H5N1 流感病毒交叉保护性免疫

Neuraminidase-based cross-protective immunity against H5N1 influenza viruses in humans
作者： Iván Sanz-Muñoz, Carlos J. Ciria-Gil, Alejandro Martín-Toribio, Marina Toquero-Asensio, Javier Sánchez‐Martínez, Carla Rodríguez-Crespo, Marta Hernández, Iris Barragan-Martin, Sara Landeras-Bueno, Jose M. Eiros
摘要中文翻译摘要 高致病性禽流感 A(H5N1) 病毒继续在全球 野生鸟类、家禽和哺乳动物 宿主 中传播,与哺乳动物适应相关的遗传特征的检测强调需要定义与大流行防范相关的预先存在的人类免疫力。尽管血清学和 疫苗 研究历来强调血凝素 (HA);神经氨酸酶(NA)是能够提供保护性免疫的关键抗原。在这里,我们对 2009 年大流行前后 10 个流感季节收集的人类血清中的 NA 特异性抗体进行了回顾性分析。在季节性灭活流感 疫苗接种 (T1) 之前和 疫苗接种 (T2) 后 28 天,总共获得了 749 份配对血清样本(总共 1,498 份)。使用代表来自进化枝 2.3.4.4b A(H5N1) 病毒的三种基因型(B.3.13、D1.1 和 EA-2021-AB)的 N1 抗原,通过酶联凝集素测定来测量 NA 抑制抗体。HA 反应通过血凝抑制 (HAI) 进行评估,中和抗体通过基于荧光素酶的微中和进行评估。跨季节可检测到针对所有三种基因型的 NA 抑制抗体,在 疫苗接种 之前,相当大比例的个体中存在可测量的交叉反应活性。疫苗接种 适度增加了 NA 抑制滴度,特别是在 2009-2010 年将 A(H1N1)pdm09 引入 疫苗 制剂后。最高反应发生在大流行后季节,表明针对 H5N1 分支 2.3.4.4b 病毒的交叉反应性增强。相比之下,尽管通过酶联免疫吸附测定可检测到HA反应性抗体,但针对H5的HAI活性通常较低或检测不到。这些数据表明,交叉反应性 NA 特异性抗体存在于人血清中,并且可以通过季节性 疫苗接种 增强,支持在流感血清监测和 疫苗 评估中更多地考虑 NA。重要性 A(H5N1) 病毒的持续传播和哺乳动物适应标记的报道加剧了人们对潜在人畜共患 传播 传染给人类的担忧。大多数人类抗体反应研究都集中在流感 HA 上,但 NA 也是一个重要的免疫靶点。通过分析从 10 多个流感季节收集的人类配对血清,我们发现 NA 抑制抗体与来自 clade 2.3.4.4b A(H5N1) 病毒的多种当代基因型发生交叉反应,是可检测到的,并且可以被季节性灭活流感 疫苗接种 适度但持续地增强,特别是在 2009 年大流行之后。在大流行后季节观察到的交叉反应性 NA 反应的增加表明,接触 A(H1N1)pdm09 可能会增强识别当代 H5N1 病毒的免疫力。这些发现强调了在流感 疫苗接种 期间诱导 NA 特异性抗体以及将 NA 纳入 监测 以及评估 疫苗 的重要性,旨在改善对新出现的流感病毒的准备。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查人类对 H5N1 流感病毒的预先存在的免疫力,特别是考虑到 A(H5N1) 病毒在全球的持续传播及其对哺乳动物 宿主 的适应,神经氨酸酶 (NA) 作为潜在免疫靶点的作用。
设计与对象
该研究对 10 个流感季节(包括 2009 年流感大流行之前和之后的时期)收集的人类血清中的 NA 特异性抗体进行了回顾性分析,以评估针对 H5N1 分支 2.3.4.4b 病毒的交叉反应免疫。
核心方法
使用来自进化枝 2.3.4.4b A(H5N1) 病毒的三种基因型的 N1 抗原进行酶联凝集素测定来测量 NA 抑制抗体。通过血凝抑制评估HA反应,并使用基于荧光素酶的微中和评估中和抗体。
主要结果
总共分析了 749 份配对血清样本,在 疫苗接种 之前在相当大比例的个体中检测到可测量的交叉反应 NA 抑制活性。疫苗接种 适度增加了 NA 抑制滴度,尤其是在 2009 年大流行之后。
解释与创新
结果表明,交叉反应性 NA 特异性抗体存在于人血清中,并且可以通过季节性 疫苗接种 增强,表明 NA 作为血清监测和 疫苗 开发目标的潜在价值。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现支持将 NA 纳入流感 监测 和 疫苗 评估中,以更好地为新出现的 H5N1 病毒做好准备,该病毒会带来重大的人畜共患风险。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性设计和对历史血清样本的依赖而受到限制,但由于跨多个季节交叉反应 NA 抗体的一致检测以及经过验证的检测方法的使用,证据是强有力的。

Neuraminidase-based cross-protective immunity against H5N1 influenza viruses in humans

Authors: Iván Sanz-Muñoz, Carlos J. Ciria-Gil, Alejandro Martín-Toribio, Marina Toquero-Asensio, Javier Sánchez‐Martínez, Carla Rodríguez-Crespo, Marta Hernández, Iris Barragan-Martin, Sara Landeras-Bueno, Jose M. Eiros
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Highly pathogenic avian influenza A(H5N1) virus continues to expand globally across wild birds, poultry, and mammalian hosts, and the detection of genetic features associated with mammalian adaptation underscores the need to define pre-existing human immunity relevant to pandemic preparedness. Although serologic and vaccine studies have historically emphasized hemagglutinin (HA); neuraminidase (NA) is a key antigen capable of providing protective immunity. Here, we performed a retrospective analysis of NA-specific antibodies in human sera collected across 10 influenza seasons spanning the pre- and post-2009 pandemic periods. A total of 749 paired serum samples (1,498 total) were obtained prior to seasonal inactivated influenza vaccination (T1) and 28 days after vaccination (T2). NA-inhibiting antibodies were measured by enzyme-linked lectin assay using N1 antigens representing three genotypes (B.3.13, D1.1, and EA-2021-AB) from clade 2.3.4.4b A(H5N1) viruses. HA responses were assessed by hemagglutination inhibition (HAI), and neutralizing antibodies by luciferase-based microneutralization. NA-inhibiting antibodies were detectable against all three genotypes across seasons, with measurable cross-reactive activity present in a substantial proportion of individuals before vaccination. Vaccination modestly increased NA-inhibiting titers, particularly after 2009–2010 following the introduction of A(H1N1)pdm09 into vaccine formulations. The highest responses occurred during post-pandemic seasons, suggesting enhanced cross-reactivity against H5N1 clade 2.3.4.4b viruses. In contrast, HAI activity against H5 was generally low or undetectable, despite detectable HA-reactive antibodies by enzyme-linked immunosorbent assay. These data indicate that cross-reactive NA-specific antibodies are present in human sera and can be boosted by seasonal vaccination, supporting increased consideration of NA in influenza serosurveillance and vaccine evaluation. IMPORTANCE The continued spread of A(H5N1) viruses and reports of mammalian adaptation markers heighten concerns about potential zoonotic transmission into humans. Most studies of human antibody responses focus on influenza HA, but NA is also an important immune target. By analyzing paired sera from humans collected over 10 influenza seasons, we show that NA-inhibiting antibodies cross-reacting with multiple contemporary genotypes from clade 2.3.4.4b A(H5N1) viruses are detectable and can be modestly but consistently boosted by seasonal inactivated influenza vaccination, particularly after the 2009 pandemic. The increase in cross-reactive NA responses observed during post-pandemic seasons suggests that A(H1N1)pdm09 exposure may have enhanced immunity recognizing contemporary H5N1 viruses. These findings highlight the importance of inducing NA-specific antibodies during influenza vaccination and of including NA in surveillance and in the assessment of vaccines intended to improve preparedness for emerging influenza viruses.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate pre-existing human immunity against H5N1 influenza viruses, particularly focusing on the role of neuraminidase (NA) as a potential immune target given the continued global spread of A(H5N1) viruses and their adaptation to mammalian hosts.
Design and population
The study conducted a retrospective analysis of NA-specific antibodies in human sera collected across 10 influenza seasons, including periods before and after the 2009 pandemic, to assess cross-reactive immunity against H5N1 clade 2.3.4.4b viruses.
Core methods
NA-inhibiting antibodies were measured using enzyme-linked lectin assays with N1 antigens from three genotypes of clade 2.3.4.4b A(H5N1) viruses. HA responses were evaluated by hemagglutination inhibition, and neutralizing antibodies were assessed using luciferase-based microneutralization.
Main results
A total of 749 paired serum samples were analyzed, with measurable cross-reactive NA-inhibiting activity detected in a substantial proportion of individuals before vaccination. Vaccination modestly increased NA-inhibiting titers, especially after the 2009 pandemic.
Interpretation and novelty
The results suggest that cross-reactive NA-specific antibodies are present in human sera and can be boosted by seasonal vaccination, indicating the potential value of NA as a target for serosurveillance and vaccine development.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings support the inclusion of NA in influenza surveillance and vaccine evaluation to better prepare for emerging H5N1 viruses, which pose a significant zoonotic risk.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and reliance on historical serum samples, but the evidence is strong due to the consistent detection of cross-reactive NA antibodies across multiple seasons and the use of validated assays.

表面糖蛋白相容性的分子决定因素可以解释 高致病性禽流感 A 病毒的出现

Molecular determinants of surface glycoprotein compatibility may explain the emergence of highly pathogenic avian influenza A viruses
作者： Ranjana Nataraj, Ahmed Kandeil, Avinash Karkada Ashok, Konstantin Andreev, Anantika Chandra, Richard J. Webby, Sannula Kesavardhana
摘要中文翻译摘要 禽流感 病毒在不同 宿主 物种中的出现和传播,以高致病性分支 2.3.4.4b H5N1 病毒为例,部分取决于表面糖蛋白血凝素 (HA) 和神经氨酸酶 (NA) 之间的功能相容性。然而,控制 HA-NA 优先配对的分子决定因素仍不清楚。在这里,我们发现具有六个糖基化位点的进化枝 2.3.4.4b H5 HA 优先与鸟类和哺乳动物中的全长(长柄)N1 NA 配对。相比之下,来自具有七个糖基化位点的早期进化枝的 H5 HA 蛋白优先与以茎缺失(短茎)为特征的 N1 NA 蛋白配对。我们的结果表明,在 禽流感 病毒中观察到的 HA S/T160A 突变 废除了 N158 糖基化位点,有利于与长柄 N1 配对,从而改善分子相容性并增强病毒适应性。N158 聚糖是 Gs/GD 谱系 H5 病毒与短茎 N1 蛋白优先配对的基础。这些发现表明,禽流感 病毒中的 HA-NA 配对并非完全随机的过程,而是受到 HA 糖基化状态和 NA 茎长度的限制,这对病毒适应性和分支 2.3.4.4b H5N1 病毒的出现有影响。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在研究控制 禽流感 病毒中 HA-NA 配对的分子决定因素,因为这些病毒在不同 宿主 物种中的出现和传播取决于血凝素 (HA) 和神经氨酸酶 (NA) 之间的功能相容性。
设计与对象
该研究重点关注 禽流感 病毒,特别检查了分支 2.3.4.4b H5N1 病毒,并将其与早期分支的 H5N1 病毒进行比较,并分析了它们与鸟类和哺乳动物中 N1 神经氨酸酶蛋白的相互作用。
核心方法
该研究使用分子分析来比较来自 禽流感 病毒不同分支的 HA 和 NA 蛋白,识别影响它们配对和相容性的糖基化位点和 突变。
主要结果
结果显示,具有六个糖基化位点的进化枝 2.3.4.4b H5 HA 优先与全长(长柄)N1 NA 配对,而来自具有七个糖基化位点的早期进化枝的 H5 HA 蛋白优先与具有柄缺失(短柄)的 N1 NA 蛋白配对。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,禽流感 病毒中的 HA-NA 配对并不完全是随机的,而是受到 HA 糖基化状态和 NA 茎长度的影响,这对病毒适应性和分支 2.3.4.4b H5N1 病毒的出现具有影响。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果提供了对 高致病性禽流感 A 病毒出现背后的分子机制的见解,这可以为 监测、诊断 和预防策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于分子分析,不包括实验验证,这可能会限制证据的强度。然而,这些发现得到了对 HA-NA 配对差异的直接观察的支持。

Molecular determinants of surface glycoprotein compatibility may explain the emergence of highly pathogenic avian influenza A viruses

Authors: Ranjana Nataraj, Ahmed Kandeil, Avinash Karkada Ashok, Konstantin Andreev, Anantika Chandra, Richard J. Webby, Sannula Kesavardhana
Original abstract (English)
Abstract The emergence and spread of avian influenza viruses across diverse host species, exemplified by highly pathogenic clade 2.3.4.4b H5N1 viruses, depend in part on the functional compatibility between the surface glycoproteins hemagglutinin (HA) and neuraminidase (NA). However, the molecular determinants governing preferential HA-NA pairing remain unclear. Here, we show that clade 2.3.4.4b H5 HA with six glycosylation sites preferentially pairs with the full-length (long-stalk) N1 NA in birds and mammals. By contrast, H5 HA proteins from earlier clades with seven glycosylation sites preferentially pair with N1 NA proteins characterized by a stalk deletion (short-stalk). Our results indicate that the HA S/T160A mutation, observed in avian influenza viruses, abolishes the N158 glycosylation site and favors pairing with long-stalk N1, improving molecular compatibility and enhancing viral fitness. The N158 glycan underlies the preferential pairing of Gs/GD-lineage H5 viruses with short-stalk N1 proteins. These findings suggest that HA-NA pairing in avian influenza viruses is not an entirely stochastic process but is constrained by HA glycosylation status and NA stalk length, with implications for viral fitness and the emergence of clade 2.3.4.4b H5N1 viruses.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the molecular determinants governing HA-NA pairing in avian influenza viruses, as the emergence and spread of these viruses across diverse host species depend on the functional compatibility between hemagglutinin (HA) and neuraminidase (NA).
Design and population
The study focused on avian influenza viruses, specifically examining clade 2.3.4.4b H5N1 viruses and comparing them with H5N1 viruses from earlier clades, as well as analyzing their interactions with N1 neuraminidase proteins in birds and mammals.
Core methods
The study used molecular analysis to compare HA and NA proteins from different clades of avian influenza viruses, identifying glycosylation sites and mutations that influence their pairing and compatibility.
Main results
The results showed that clade 2.3.4.4b H5 HA with six glycosylation sites preferentially pairs with the full-length (long-stalk) N1 NA, whereas H5 HA proteins from earlier clades with seven glycosylation sites preferentially pair with N1 NA proteins with a stalk deletion (short-stalk).
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that HA-NA pairing in avian influenza viruses is not entirely stochastic but is influenced by HA glycosylation status and NA stalk length, which has implications for viral fitness and the emergence of clade 2.3.4.4b H5N1 viruses.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results provide insights into the molecular mechanisms underlying the emergence of highly pathogenic avian influenza A viruses, which could inform surveillance, diagnosis, and prevention strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on molecular analysis and does not include experimental validation, which may limit the strength of the evidence. However, the findings are supported by direct observations of HA-NA pairing differences.

卵内注射表达 HA1 的乳酸乳球菌可通过增强肠道发育和免疫功能来保护雏鸡免受 H9N2 感染

In ovo administration of HA1-expressing Lactococcus lactis protects chicks against H9N2 infection via enhancing intestinal development and immune function
作者： Jiaxin Yuan, Tingting Liu, Dayi Liu, Tong Xu, LI Longfei, Longfei Li
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估使用表达 H9N2 禽流感 病毒血凝素 (HA) 亚基 1 (HA1) 蛋白的重组乳酸乳球菌作为一种新型卵内 疫苗 蛋白来保护雏鸡免受 H9N2 感染的潜力。
设计与对象
该研究涉及 160 个存活的含胚卵,随机分配到四组,这些卵接种了 PBS、灭活的 H9N2 疫苗、空乳酸乳球菌 媒介 或表达 HA1 的重组乳酸乳球菌。通过in ovo 疫苗接种进行评价。
核心方法
通过评估 H9N2 病毒攻击后雏鸡的临床表现、体重减轻、病毒脱落滴度和肺部病变,评估重组乳酸乳球菌-HA1 的功效。此外,还测量了血清HI滴度、HA特异性IgG、IL-2、IL-4、IFN-γ、粘膜SIgA浓度和淋巴细胞增殖反应等免疫相关指标。
主要结果
结果表明,卵内注射重组乳酸乳球菌-HA1可显着提高平均日增重(ADG)、饲料转化率(FCR)以及胸腺、脾脏、法氏囊等免疫指标,血清HI滴度,HA特异性IgG、IL-2、IL-4、IFN-γ以及粘膜SIgA浓度。它还增加了十二指肠和空肠中的 VH 和 VH/CD,并上调了肠屏障相关基因的转录。与 PBS 组和 L1 组相比,重组乳酸乳球菌-HA1 组的雏鸡临床症状较轻,体重减轻较少,病毒脱落滴度较低。
解释与创新
作者解释说,在蛋内施用表达 HA1 的重组乳酸乳球菌可加速早期肠道成熟,并在肉鸡中诱导 HA1 特异性适应性 免疫应答,这可能提供与灭活的 疫苗 类似的保护作用。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,这种方法可能是开发针对 H9N2 疫苗 病毒的新型卵内 疫苗 的有前途的策略,有可能提高 疫苗 的功效,并为家禽疾病预防提供新方法。
局限与证据强度
该研究的证据基于有限数量的胚胎,不包括长期随访或更广泛的 宿主 人群,这可能会影响结果的普遍性。由于受控实验设置和缺乏外部验证,证据强度中等。

In ovo administration of HA1-expressing Lactococcus lactis protects chicks against H9N2 infection via enhancing intestinal development and immune function

Authors: Jiaxin Yuan, Tingting Liu, Dayi Liu, Tong Xu, LI Longfei, Longfei Li
Original abstract (English)
In this study, we utilized recombinant Lactococcus lactis (L. lactis) expressing the H9N2 AIV hemagglutinin (HA) subunit 1 (HA1) protein and evaluated its efficacy through in ovo vaccination. At embryonic day 17.5, 160 viable embryonated eggs were randomly assigned to four groups and inoculated with either PBS, an inactivated H9N2 vaccine, an empty L. lactis vector, or recombinant L. The results showed that no significant differences in hatchability were observed across all groups (p > 0.05). In ovo injection of recombinant L. lactis- HA1 markedly increased average daily gain (ADG), improved feed conversion ratio (FCR) (p < 0.05), and elevated indices for the thymus, spleen and bursa of Fabricius, and elevated serum HI titers, HA-specific IgG, IL-2, IL-4, and IFN-γ, as well as increased mucosal SIgA concentrations and strengthened HA1-specific lymphocyte proliferative responses (p < 0.05). Furthermore, this treatment increased VH and the VH/CD in both the duodenum and jejunum, and upregulated the transcriptional levels of intestinal barrier-associated genes (p < 0.05). Following H9N2 viral challenge, chicks in the recombinant L. lactis -HA1 group exhibited milder clinical manifestations and less body weight loss, along with numerically lower viral shedding titers and less pronounced pulmonary lesions compared with the PBS and L1 groups. Notably, it conferred protective effects that trended toward a level similar to that of the inactivated vaccine. Collectively, in ovo administration of recombinant L. lactis expressing HA1 accelerates early intestinal maturation, potentially in part through the intrinsic probiotic properties of L. lactis, and additionally induces HA1‐specific adaptive immune responses in broiler chicks. These findings provide a theoretical basis for developing novel in‐ovo lactic‐acid‐bacterium‐vectored vaccines against H9N2 avian influenza virus.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to evaluate the potential of using recombinant Lactococcus lactis expressing the H9N2 avian influenza virus hemagglutinin (HA) subunit 1 (HA1) protein as a novel in ovo vaccine to protect chicks against H9N2 infection.
Design and population
The study involved 160 viable embryonated eggs randomly assigned to four groups, which were inoculated with either PBS, an inactivated H9N2 vaccine, an empty Lactococcus lactis vector, or recombinant Lactococcus lactis expressing HA1. The evaluation was conducted through in ovo vaccination.
Core methods
The efficacy of the recombinant Lactococcus lactis- HA1 was assessed by evaluating clinical manifestations, body weight loss, viral shedding titers, and pulmonary lesions in chicks following H9N2 viral challenge. Additionally, immune-related indices such as serum HI titers, HA-specific IgG, IL-2, IL-4, IFN-γ, mucosal SIgA concentrations, and lymphocyte proliferative responses were measured.
Main results
The results showed that in ovo injection of recombinant Lactococcus lactis-HA1 significantly improved average daily gain (ADG), feed conversion ratio (FCR), and immune indices such as thymus, spleen, and bursa of Fabricius weights, serum HI titers, HA-specific IgG, IL-2, IL-4, and IFN-γ, as well as mucosal SIgA concentrations. It also increased VH and VH/CD in the duodenum and jejunum and upregulated intestinal barrier-associated gene transcription. Chicks in the recombinant Lactococcus lactis-HA1 group exhibited milder clinical symptoms, less body weight loss, and lower viral shedding titers compared to the PBS and L1 groups.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that the in ovo administration of recombinant Lactococcus lactis expressing HA1 accelerates early intestinal maturation and induces HA1-specific adaptive immune responses in broiler chicks, which may offer protective effects similar to those of the inactivated vaccine.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that this approach could be a promising strategy for developing novel in ovo vaccines against H9N2 avian influenza virus, potentially improving vaccine efficacy and providing a new method for poultry disease prevention.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's evidence is based on a limited number of embryos and does not include long-term follow-up or broader host populations, which may affect the generalizability of the results. The evidence strength is moderate due to the controlled experimental setting and the lack of external validation.

澳大利亚不断扩大的 H5N1 野生动物 暴发:传统防备能力不足以应对的大流行病的教训

Australia's Expanding H5N1 Wildlife Outbreak: Lessons From a Panzootic That Has Outgrown Conventional Preparedness
作者： Md Jisan Ahmed, Ritu Chalise, Prajwal Bhandari, Fabrício Souza Campos
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
这项研究满足了了解 H5N1 禽流感 的传播及其对全球健康的影响的需要,强调了该病毒超越传统地理障碍的能力。
设计与对象
该研究描述了在澳大利亚各地的候鸟(包括棕色贼鸥和南方巨海燕)中检测到的 H5N1 进化枝 2.3.4.4b,并将其起源追溯到南印度洋的赫德岛。
核心方法
进行基因组分析以确定检测到的病毒与赫德岛传播的病毒之间的关系,表明独立入侵而不是本地 传播。
主要结果
澳大利亚首次在大陆检测到 H5N1 进化枝 2.3.4.4b 是在棕色贼鸥和南方巨海燕身上,基因组证据表明这些病毒与赫德岛上的病毒关系最密切。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,该病毒有能力通过相互关联的野生动物种群跨大陆和海洋传播,标志着其洲际扩张进入了一个新阶段。
科研与公卫意义
该研究提供了对 监测 和 H5N1 禽流感 所需的准备工作的重要见解,强调了监测候鸟种群以进行早期发现的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究由于依赖于少数已发现病例的基因组数据而受到限制,但由于与赫德岛病毒有明确的遗传关系,因此证据确凿。

Australia's Expanding H5N1 Wildlife Outbreak: Lessons From a Panzootic That Has Outgrown Conventional Preparedness

Authors: Md Jisan Ahmed, Ritu Chalise, Prajwal Bhandari, Fabrício Souza Campos
Original abstract (English)
Genomic analyses indicate that these viruses were most closely related to those circulating on Heard Island in the southern Indian Ocean and suggest independent incursions from the sub‐Antarctic region rather than local transmission between the birds involved in the earliest detections [1, 2, 3].This finding marks another stage in the extraordinary intercontinental expansion of H5N1 clade 2.3.4.4b. Question and background
This study addresses the need to understand the spread of H5N1 avian influenza and its implications for global health, highlighting the virus's ability to expand beyond traditional geographic barriers.
Design and population
The study describes the detection of H5N1 clade 2.3.4.4b in migratory birds across Australia, including a brown skua and a southern giant petrel, and traces its origin to Heard Island in the southern Indian Ocean.
Core methods
Genomic analyses were conducted to determine the relationship between the detected viruses and those circulating in Heard Island, suggesting independent incursions rather than local transmission.
Main results
The first confirmed mainland detection of H5N1 clade 2.3.4.4b in Australia was in a brown skua and a southern giant petrel, with genomic evidence indicating these viruses were most closely related to those on Heard Island.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings demonstrate the virus's capacity to spread across continents and oceans through interconnected wildlife populations, marking a new stage in its intercontinental expansion.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study provides critical insights into the surveillance and preparedness needed for H5N1 avian influenza, emphasizing the importance of monitoring migratory bird populations for early detection.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on genomic data from a small number of detected cases, but the evidence is strong due to the clear genetic relationship to viruses on Heard Island.

脂质纳米粒子封装的 DNA 疫苗 在鸡中诱导针对 禽流感 病毒的强大而持久的免疫力

Lipid nanoparticle-encapsulated DNA vaccine induces robust and durable immunity against avian influenza viruses in chickens
作者： Sushmita Kumari, Kassandra Durazo-Martínez, The N. Nguyen, Hung Q. Luong, Jayeshbhai Chaudhari, Danh Cong Lai, Mary Drozd, Hiep L. X. Vu
摘要中文翻译为了评估脂质纳米颗粒 (LNP) 封装的 DNA 疫苗 对鸡中 禽流感 病毒的免疫原性,将来自三种替代低致病性 禽流感 病毒 (LPAIV) 亚型 H2N2、H5N3 和 H6N8 的血凝素 (HA) 基因克隆到表达质粒中,并配制为单价或二价 LNP-pDNA 疫苗。单次肌肉注射 5 或 15 μg 单价 LNP-pDNA 疫苗 在鸡体内诱导强烈的血凝抑制 (HI) 抗体反应,滴度分别超过 1:64 和 1:128,而同等剂量的裸露 DNA 未能引发可检测到的 HI 抗体。含有 H2 和 H6 HA 基因的二价 LNP-pDNA 疫苗 引发针对这两种亚型的 HI 抗体反应。此外,用编码 H5 抗原的 LNP-pDNA 疫苗 进行单次免疫可诱导持久的抗体反应,HI 滴度高于 1:32,可维持近六个月。在 15 μg 剂量下,LNP-pDNA 疫苗 显着减少了呼吸道分泌物中的病毒脱落,并防止了同源 LPAIV H5N3 病毒攻击后肺部可检测到的病毒感染。总体而言,LNP 封装显着增强了 pDNA 疫苗 在家禽中的免疫原性。因此,这项研究提供了证据证明 LNP-pDNA 可以成为控制家禽中 禽流感 病毒的安全有效的策略。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估脂质纳米颗粒 (LNP) 封装的 DNA 疫苗 对鸡中 禽流感 病毒的免疫原性,重点关注低致病性 禽流感 病毒 (LPAIV) 亚型。
设计与对象
该研究涉及给鸡单次肌肉注射 5 或 15 μg 单价 LNP-pDNA 疫苗,并与裸露 DNA 疫苗 进行比较。测试了二价 疫苗 诱导针对两种亚型 H2 和 H6 的免疫力的能力。
核心方法
疫苗 使用 LPAIV 亚型的 HA 基因配制,并测试其诱导血凝抑制 (HI) 抗体反应的能力。进行了同源 H5N3 病毒的攻击研究以评估 疫苗 的功效。
主要结果
在 15 μg 剂量下,LNP-pDNA 疫苗 显着减少了呼吸道分泌物中的病毒脱落,并防止了同源 LPAIV H5N3 病毒攻击后肺部可检测到的病毒感染。二价 疫苗 诱导针对 H2 和 H6 亚型的 HI 抗体应答。
解释与创新
结果表明,LNP-pDNA 疫苗 可以在鸡中诱导针对 禽流感 病毒的强大而持久的免疫力,为 疫苗 在家禽中的开发提供了一种新方法。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,LNP-pDNA 疫苗 可能是控制家禽 禽流感 的宝贵工具,有可能降低人畜共患 传播 的风险并增强疾病预防策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于单剂量和一组特定的 LPAIV 亚型,但由于与裸露 DNA 的直接比较和挑战模型的使用,证据很充分。

Lipid nanoparticle-encapsulated DNA vaccine induces robust and durable immunity against avian influenza viruses in chickens

Authors: Sushmita Kumari, Kassandra Durazo-Martínez, The N. Nguyen, Hung Q. Luong, Jayeshbhai Chaudhari, Danh Cong Lai, Mary Drozd, Hiep L. X. Vu
Original abstract (English)
To evaluate the immunogenicity of lipid nanoparticle (LNP)-encapsulated DNA vaccines against avian influenza virus in chickens, hemagglutinin (HA) genes from three surrogate low pathogenic avian influenza virus (LPAIV) subtypes, H2N2, H5N3, and H6N8, were cloned into an expression plasmid and formulated as monovalent or bivalent LNP-pDNA vaccines. A single intramuscular dose of 5 or 15 μg of monovalent LNP-pDNA vaccines induced strong hemagglutination inhibition (HI) antibody responses in chickens, with titers exceeding 1:64 and 1:128 respectively, whereas equivalent doses of naked DNA failed to elicit detectable HI antibodies. The bivalent LNP-pDNA vaccine containing H2 and H6 HA genes elicited HI antibody responses against both subtypes. Furthermore, a single immunization with the LNP-pDNA vaccine encoding the H5 antigen induced durable antibody responses, with HI titers above 1:32 maintained for nearly six months. At the 15-μg dose, the LNP-pDNA vaccine significantly reduced viral shedding in respiratory secretions and prevented detectable viral infection in the lungs following challenge with the homologous LPAIV H5N3 virus. Overall, LNP encapsulation markedly enhances the immunogenicity of pDNA vaccines in poultry. Thus, this study provides a proof-of-evidence that LNP-pDNA can be a safe and effective strategy for controlling avian influenza viruses in poultry.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to evaluate the immunogenicity of lipid nanoparticle (LNP)-encapsulated DNA vaccines against avian influenza virus in chickens, focusing on low pathogenic avian influenza virus (LPAIV) subtypes.
Design and population
The study involved administering a single intramuscular dose of 5 or 15 μg of monovalent LNP-pDNA vaccines to chickens, with comparisons made to naked DNA vaccines. The bivalent vaccine was tested for its ability to induce immunity against two subtypes, H2 and H6.
Core methods
The vaccines were formulated using HA genes from LPAIV subtypes and tested for their ability to induce hemagglutination inhibition (HI) antibody responses. A challenge study with the homologous H5N3 virus was conducted to assess vaccine efficacy.
Main results
At the 15-μg dose, the LNP-pDNA vaccine significantly reduced viral shedding in respiratory secretions and prevented detectable viral infection in the lungs following challenge with the homologous LPAIV H5N3 virus. The bivalent vaccine induced HI antibody responses against both H2 and H6 subtypes.
Interpretation and novelty
The results demonstrate that LNP-pDNA vaccines can induce strong and durable immunity against avian influenza viruses in chickens, offering a novel approach for vaccine development in poultry.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that LNP-pDNA vaccines could be a valuable tool for controlling avian influenza in poultry, potentially reducing the risk of zoonotic transmission and enhancing disease prevention strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to a single dose and a specific set of LPAIV subtypes, but the evidence is strong due to the direct comparison with naked DNA and the use of a challenge model.

2023 年智利野生水獭中的 死亡率 与高致病性禽流感 (H5N1) 相关

Mortality in Wild Otters Linked to HPAI (H5N1) in Chile, 2023
作者： Gonzalo Medina‐Vogel, Mauricio Ulloa, Gabriela Muñoz Brito, Naomi Ariyama, Carolina Aguayo, Magdalena Johow, Catalina Pardo‐Roa, Eva Sierra, Ana Colom-Rivero, Antonio Fernández
摘要中文翻译本研究记录了 2023 年在智利野生海獭 (Lontra felina) 和南方河獭 (Lontra provocax) 中对 高致病性禽流感 A(H5N1) 分支 2.3.4.4b 的分子检测。对官方 SERNAPESCA 搁浅记录的分析发现,与往年相比,2023 年报告的水獭 死亡率 有所增加,在地理和时间上与野生鸟类 和哺乳动物中的区域性 H5N1 暴发。5 只水獭的 6 份样本的 RT-qPCR 结果呈 H5N1 阳性。对恢复的病毒序列的系统发育分析与南美洲 暴发 期间在智利和秘鲁传播的病毒的遗传多样性一致。这些分子发现和流行病学观察结果支持 H5N1 病毒传播与报道的水獭 死亡率 之间的关联,但并未确定个体动物的死亡原因或因感染而死亡的比例。综合野生动物 监测 有必要更好地了解 H5N1 对这些脆弱物种的潜在影响。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在调查 野生鸟类 中的 2023 H5N1 暴发 与哺乳动物之间的潜在联系以及智利野生水獭中 死亡率 的增加,以满足了解高致病性禽流感 (H5N1) 对海洋和河獭种群的生态和公共卫生影响的需要。
设计与对象
该研究分析了智利官方 SERNAPESCA 搁浅记录,以确定 2023 年水獭 死亡率 的增加,这在地理和时间上与 野生鸟类 和哺乳动物中的区域性 H5N1 暴发 一致。在六个样本中对五只水獭进行了测试。
核心方法
使用 RT-qPCR 对五只水獭的标本进行 H5N1 分子检测,并对恢复的病毒序列进行系统发育分析,以评估其与南美 暴发 期间在智利和秘鲁传播的病毒的遗传关系。
主要结果
对搁浅记录的分析显示,与前几年相比,2023 年报告的水獭 死亡率 有所增加,与区域性 H5N1 暴发 一致。5 只水獭的 6 份样本的 RT-qPCR 结果呈 H5N1 阳性。病毒序列的系统发育分析与 暴发 期间在智利和秘鲁传播的病毒的遗传多样性一致。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,H5N1 病毒的传播与水獭 死亡率 之间可能存在关联,但并未证实死亡原因或因感染导致的死亡比例,这凸显了进一步开展针对性研究的必要性。
科研与公卫意义
结果强调了综合野生动物 监测 的重要性,以更好地了解 H5N1 对脆弱水獭物种的影响,并为监测人畜共患流感 传播 的公共卫生策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究依赖于有限数量样本的观察数据和分子测试,这可能无法完全确定因果关系或量化 H5N1 对水獭 死亡率 的贡献。由于这些限制,证据的强度中等。

Mortality in Wild Otters Linked to HPAI (H5N1) in Chile, 2023

Authors: Gonzalo Medina‐Vogel, Mauricio Ulloa, Gabriela Muñoz Brito, Naomi Ariyama, Carolina Aguayo, Magdalena Johow, Catalina Pardo‐Roa, Eva Sierra, Ana Colom-Rivero, Antonio Fernández
Original abstract (English)
This study documents molecular detection of highly pathogenic avian influenza A(H5N1) clade 2.3.4.4b in wild marine otters (Lontra felina) and a Southern river otter (Lontra provocax) in Chile in 2023. Analysis of official SERNAPESCA stranding records identified an increase in reported otter mortality in 2023 relative to previous years, coinciding geographically and temporally with the regional H5N1 outbreak in wild birds and mammals. Five individual otters yielded H5N1-positive RT-qPCR results across six specimens. Phylogenetic analyses of recovered viral sequences were consistent with the genetic diversity of viruses circulating in Chile and Peru during the South American outbreak. These molecular findings and epidemiological observations support an association between H5N1 circulation and reported otter mortality, but do not establish the cause of death of individual animals or the proportion of deaths attributable to infection. Integrated wildlife surveillance is warranted to better understand the potential impact of H5N1 on these vulnerable species.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to investigate the potential link between the 2023 H5N1 outbreak in wild birds and mammals and increased mortality in wild otters in Chile, addressing the need to understand the ecological and public health implications of HPAI (H5N1) in marine and river otter populations.
Design and population
The study analyzed official SERNAPESCA stranding records from Chile to identify an increase in otter mortality in 2023, which coincided geographically and temporally with the regional H5N1 outbreak in wild birds and mammals. Five individual otters were tested across six specimens.
Core methods
Molecular detection of H5N1 was performed using RT-qPCR on specimens from five otters, and phylogenetic analyses were conducted on the recovered viral sequences to assess their genetic relationship to viruses circulating in Chile and Peru during the South American outbreak.
Main results
Analysis of stranding records showed an increase in reported otter mortality in 2023 relative to previous years, coinciding with the regional H5N1 outbreak. Five individual otters yielded H5N1-positive RT-qPCR results across six specimens. Phylogenetic analyses of the viral sequences were consistent with the genetic diversity of viruses circulating in Chile and Peru during the outbreak.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest a possible association between H5N1 circulation and otter mortality, but they do not confirm the cause of death or the proportion of deaths attributable to the infection, highlighting the need for further targeted research.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results emphasize the importance of integrated wildlife surveillance to better understand the impact of H5N1 on vulnerable otter species and to inform public health strategies for monitoring zoonotic influenza transmission.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study relies on observational data and molecular testing of a limited number of specimens, which may not fully establish causality or quantify the contribution of H5N1 to otter mortality. The evidence is moderate in strength due to these limitations.

基于 pH 值灭活未经巴氏消毒的牛奶中的高致病性 H5N1 病毒

pH-based inactivation of highly pathogenic H5N1 virus in unpasteurized milk
作者： A. J. Campbell, Valerie M. Le Sage, Kayla N. Brizuela, Seema S. Lakdawala
摘要中文翻译摘要 2024 年 3 月,高致病性禽流感 H5N1 的 暴发 在奶牛中得到证实,随后传播到美国各地的奶牛群,导致人类和其他动物出现 跨物种溢出 病例。受感染的奶牛生产的牛奶已被证明具有高病毒滴度,由于缺乏 治疗 以及在通常向环境开放的废水系统中的处理,对奶牛场周围的野生动物构成潜在威胁。在这里,我们研究了一种在未经巴氏消毒的牛奶中灭活 H5N1 病毒的方法,该方法利用低 pH 诱导的血凝素 (HA) 糖蛋白的不可逆构象变化。确定了三种不同甲型流感病毒完全灭活所需的 pH 值,以便随后用于酸化牛奶。使用常见的、现成的酸化溶液(如醋)表明,最终浓度为 3% 的醋(pH 3.6)在 60 分钟后可诱导未经巴氏消毒的牛奶中的 H5N1 完全失活。我们的研究结果表明,在未经巴氏消毒的牛奶中基于 pH 值灭活 H5N1 是一种快速、可行的策略,可在奶牛场处置受污染的牛奶之前使用,从而降低环境污染和农场周围动物接触的风险。重要性 经历 高致病性禽流感 H5N1 病毒的奶牛场会产生大量含有高滴度传染性 H5N1 病毒的牛奶。虽然来自病牛的牛奶不会进入消费者供应链,但通过现场废水系统处理这种未经处理的受污染牛奶仍然对环境污染和家中野生动物暴露造成重大风险,可能导致 跨物种溢出 事件。在处理牛奶之前采用一种简单的方法灭活牛奶中的病毒可以减轻这种风险。我们证明,用市售醋降低载有病毒的牛奶的 pH 值可以在 1 小时内中和奶牛 H5N1 病毒,为乳品厂在处理前灭活牛奶中的病毒提供了一种快速有效的策略。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
2024 年 3 月,奶牛中检测到 高致病性禽流感 H5N1 的 暴发 引起了人们对牛奶中病毒污染对家境野生动物和人类的潜在威胁的担忧。该研究旨在解决对未经巴氏消毒牛奶中 H5N1 病毒灭活的有效方法的需求。
设计与对象
该研究调查了使用 pH 值控制对未经巴氏消毒的牛奶中 H5N1 病毒的灭活情况。它主要针对发生 H5N1 暴发 的美国奶牛场,并且研究是在实验室环境中进行的。
核心方法
研究人员确定了完全灭活三种不同甲型流感病毒(包括 H5N1)所需的 pH 值,并测试了醋等酸化溶液的有效性。他们通过评估受控 pH 条件下感染性的丧失来测量病毒灭活情况。
主要结果
60 分钟后,最终浓度为 3% 的醋(pH 3.6)可诱导未经巴氏消毒的牛奶中的 H5N1 病毒完全失活。这种方法在一小时内就能有效中和病毒。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,使用醋进行基于 pH 值的灭活是在牛奶处理前灭活牛奶中 H5N1 病毒的一种快速且可行的策略,为降低环境污染风险提供了实用的解决方案。
科研与公卫意义
该方法可以为奶牛场提供安全管理受污染牛奶的策略,从而降低 H5N1 病毒 传播 对家境野生动物和人类的风险。
局限与证据强度
该研究是在受控实验室环境中进行的,这可能会限制其在现实农场环境中的适用性。然而,结果得到了直接实验证据的支持,并表明对该方法的有效性具有高度的信心。

pH-based inactivation of highly pathogenic H5N1 virus in unpasteurized milk

Authors: A. J. Campbell, Valerie M. Le Sage, Kayla N. Brizuela, Seema S. Lakdawala
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT An outbreak of highly pathogenic avian influenza H5N1 was confirmed in dairy cattle in March 2024 and subsequently spread to dairy herds throughout the United States, resulting in spillover cases in humans and other animals. Milk produced by infected dairy cattle, which has been shown to harbor high viral titers, poses a potential threat to peridomestic wildlife around dairies due to lack of treatment and disposal in wastewater systems that are often open to the environment. Here, we investigated a method for inactivating H5N1 virus in unpasteurized milk, utilizing the irreversible conformational change of the hemagglutinin (HA) glycoprotein induced by low pH. The pH needed for complete inactivation of three distinct influenza A viruses was determined for subsequent use in acidifying milk. The use of common, readily available acidification solutions like vinegar demonstrated that a 3% final concentration of vinegar (pH 3.6) induced complete inactivation of H5N1 in unpasteurized milk after 60 min. Our results suggest that pH-based inactivation of H5N1 in unpasteurized milk is a quick, feasible strategy that could be used on dairy farms prior to disposal of contaminated milk, thus reducing the risks of environmental contamination and exposure of animals around farms. IMPORTANCE Dairy farms experiencing outbreaks of highly pathogenic avian influenza H5N1 generate large volumes of milk with high titers of infectious H5N1 virus. While milk from sick cows does not enter the consumer supply chain, disposal of this untreated, contaminated milk through on-site wastewater systems still poses a significant risk for environmental contamination and exposure of peridomestic wildlife, potentially leading to spillover events. A simple method for inactivation of virus in milk prior to disposal could mitigate this risk. We show that lowering the pH of virus-laden milk with commercially available vinegar can neutralize dairy cattle H5N1 virus within 1 h, providing a quick and effective strategy for potential use on dairies to inactivate the virus in milk before disposal.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
An outbreak of highly pathogenic avian influenza H5N1 in dairy cattle in March 2024 raised concerns about the potential threat of viral contamination in milk to peridomestic wildlife and humans. The study aimed to address the need for an effective method to inactivate H5N1 virus in unpasteurized milk.
Design and population
The study investigated the inactivation of H5N1 virus in unpasteurized milk using pH manipulation. It focused on dairy farms in the United States where H5N1 outbreaks had occurred, and the research was conducted in a laboratory setting.
Core methods
The researchers determined the pH required for complete inactivation of three distinct influenza A viruses, including H5N1, and tested the effectiveness of acidification solutions like vinegar. They measured viral inactivation by assessing the loss of infectivity under controlled pH conditions.
Main results
A 3% final concentration of vinegar (pH 3.6) induced complete inactivation of H5N1 virus in unpasteurized milk after 60 minutes. This method was effective in neutralizing the virus within one hour.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings demonstrate that pH-based inactivation using vinegar is a quick and feasible strategy for deactivating H5N1 virus in milk before disposal, offering a practical solution for reducing environmental contamination risks.
Scientific and public-health significance
This method could inform strategies for dairy farms to safely manage contaminated milk, thereby reducing the risk of H5N1 virus transmission to peridomestic wildlife and humans.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was conducted in a controlled laboratory environment, which may limit its applicability to real-world farm settings. However, the results are supported by direct experimental evidence and suggest a high level of confidence in the method's effectiveness.

邻近蛋白质组学揭示了由 禽流感 聚合酶的哺乳动物适应性 PB2-E627K 突变 驱动的相互作用组重新布线

Proximity Proteomics Reveals Interactome Rewiring Driven by the Mammalian-Adaptive PB2-E627K Mutation of Avian Influenza Polymerase
作者： Wenyue Lou, Kai-Rong Zhang, Ming-Yang Zhang, Yue-Hui Zhang, Chao-Zhi Jin, Li-Min Shang, Xiang-Zheng Liu, Shan-Shan Wu, Yu-Chen Liu, Jian Wang
摘要中文翻译摘要:甲型流感病毒聚合酶中的聚合酶碱性蛋白 2 (PB2) E627K 取代是哺乳动物适应的关键决定因素,但其对 宿主 相互作用的影响仍不完全清楚。在人类细胞中使用 TurboID 邻近标记,我们在分离的 PB2 和三聚体 (PA-PB1-PB2) 环境中分析了与野生型和 E627K 突变 H5N1 聚合酶相关的 宿主 蛋白,识别了 204 个高可信度的邻近相互作用。聚合酶组装驱动 PB2 相关的相互作用组,与单独的 PB2 相比,在三聚体环境中检测到的 宿主 相互作用显着扩展。E627K 重塑 宿主 关联,改变与细胞结构组织和细胞内运输相关的相互作用,同时保留核运输和 RNP 组装。WT 相关但 E627K 丢失的蛋白质与 宿主 RNA 加工和蛋白质质量控​​制途径相关。差异相互作用评分将聚合酶组装依赖性相互作用与单独的 PB2 关联区分开来。免疫共沉淀和 AlphaFold3 的整合为代表性相互作用提供了补充证据,并揭示了 宿主 因子通过 PB2 以外的亚基与聚合酶结合,强调了组装复合物对于相互作用识别的重要性。总之,这些发现表明 PB2-E627K 在保守的功能景观中选择性地重新分配相互作用偏好。这项研究为了解流感病毒适应提供了资源,并确定了候选 宿主 因素以供进一步研究。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查 PB2-E627K 突变 对 禽流感 A 病毒的 宿主 相互作用的影响,因为该 突变 是哺乳动物适应的关键决定因素,但其对 宿主 相互作用的影响仍未完全了解。
设计与对象
该研究使用人类细胞中的 TurboID 邻近标记来分析与分离的 PB2 和三聚体 (PA-PB1-PB2) 环境中野生型和 E627K 突变 H5N1 聚合酶相关的 宿主 蛋白,识别出 204 个高可信度的邻近相互作用。
核心方法
研究人员在人体细胞中采用 TurboID 邻近标记,并将免疫共沉淀与 AlphaFold3 集成,为代表性相互作用提供补充证据,并揭示 宿主 因子通过 PB2 以外的亚基与聚合酶结合。
主要结果
聚合酶组装驱动 PB2 相关的相互作用组,与单独的 PB2 相比,在三聚体环境中检测到的 宿主 相互作用显着扩展。E627K 重塑 宿主 关联,改变与细胞结构组织和细胞内运输相关的相互作用,同时保留核运输和 RNP 组装。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,PB2-E627K 突变 在保守的功能景观内选择性地重新分配相互作用偏好,凸显了组装的复合物对于相互作用识别的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
该研究为了解流感病毒适应性提供了资源,并确定了候选 宿主 因子以供进一步研究,这可以为 监测、诊断 和 禽流感 的治疗策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于抽象证据,结果基于通过 TurboID 邻近标记和 AlphaFold3 集成识别的高置信度邻近相互作用,为观察到的结果提供了适度的置信度。

Proximity Proteomics Reveals Interactome Rewiring Driven by the Mammalian-Adaptive PB2-E627K Mutation of Avian Influenza Polymerase

Authors: Wenyue Lou, Kai-Rong Zhang, Ming-Yang Zhang, Yue-Hui Zhang, Chao-Zhi Jin, Li-Min Shang, Xiang-Zheng Liu, Shan-Shan Wu, Yu-Chen Liu, Jian Wang
Original abstract (English)
Abstract The polymerase basic protein 2 (PB2) E627K substitution in the influenza A virus polymerase is a key determinant of mammalian adaptation, yet its impact on the host interaction remains incompletely understood. Using TurboID proximity labeling in human cells, we profiled host proteins associated with wild-type and E627K mutant H5N1 polymerase in both isolated PB2 and trimeric (PA-PB1-PB2) contexts, identifying 204 high-confidence proximal interactions. Polymerase assembly drives the PB2-associated interactome, with substantially expanded host interactions detected in the trimeric context compared to PB2 alone. E627K remodels host associations, shifting interactions related to cellular structural organization and intracellular trafficking while preserving nuclear transport and RNP assembly. WT-associated but E627K-lost proteins relate to host RNA processing and protein quality control pathways. Differential interaction scoring distinguished polymerase assembly dependent interactions from PB2-alone associations. Integration of coimmunoprecipitation and AlphaFold3 provided complementary evidence for representative interactions and revealed host factors engage polymerase through subunits other than PB2, highlighting the importance of the assembled complex for interaction recognition. Together, these findings demonstrate that PB2-E627K selectively redistributes interaction preferences within a conserved functional landscape. This study provides a resource for understanding influenza virus adaptation and identifies candidate host factors for further investigation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the impact of the PB2-E627K mutation on host interactions of avian influenza A viruses, as this mutation is a key determinant of mammalian adaptation but its effects on host interaction remain incompletely understood.
Design and population
The study used TurboID proximity labeling in human cells to profile host proteins associated with wild-type and E627K mutant H5N1 polymerase in both isolated PB2 and trimeric (PA-PB1-PB2) contexts, identifying 204 high-confidence proximal interactions.
Core methods
The researchers employed TurboID proximity labeling in human cells and integrated coimmunoprecipitation with AlphaFold3 to provide complementary evidence for representative interactions and reveal host factors engaging the polymerase through subunits other than PB2.
Main results
Polymerase assembly drives the PB2-associated interactome, with substantially expanded host interactions detected in the trimeric context compared to PB2 alone. E627K remodels host associations, shifting interactions related to cellular structural organization and intracellular trafficking while preserving nuclear transport and RNP assembly.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings demonstrate that the PB2-E627K mutation selectively redistributes interaction preferences within a conserved functional landscape, highlighting the importance of the assembled complex for interaction recognition.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study provides a resource for understanding influenza virus adaptation and identifies candidate host factors for further investigation, which could inform surveillance, diagnosis, and therapeutic strategies for avian influenza.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to abstract-only evidence, and the results are based on high-confidence proximal interactions identified through TurboID proximity labeling and AlphaFold3 integration, providing moderate confidence in the observed findings.

GPT-5 和 Gemini 3 Flash 对 H5N1 One Health 问题的语义重复性和再现性

Semantic repeatability and reproducibility of GPT-5 and Gemini 3 Flash on H5N1 One Health questions
作者： Luizza Dalla Pria, Amelia L. Nelson, renata ivanek
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
自 2024 年以来,高致病性禽流感 A(H5N1) 重新成为 One Health 关注的焦点,在 暴发 期间,公众越来越多地咨询大语言模型 (LLM) 聊天机器人以获取健康信息。
设计与对象
在有意义地评估聊天机器人的准确性之前,必须首先了解其一致性,以便可以相信任何单个响应都可以作为模型行为的公平代表。
核心方法
我们通过采用和扩展基于质心的评估框架,在来自社交媒体话语的 15 个 H5N1 One Health 问题上描述了 GPT-5 (OpenAI) 和 Gemini 3 Flash (Google) 的语义重复性和再现性。
主要结果
每个问题提交 30 次,并使用五个思想链 (CoT) 变体重新表述,每个变体运行 30 次,产生 5,400 个答案。
解释与创新
在 MPNet 嵌入上计算成对余弦相似度,并对三个附加变压器进行敏感性分析。
科研与公卫意义
统计推断将标准非参数检验(Wilcoxon 和 Friedman 与 Holm-Bonferroni 校正配对)与嵌入间一致性统计(ICC(2,k) 和 Cronbach's $\alpha$)相结合,作为方法论的补充引入。
局限与证据强度
Both models produced high mean consistency (0.93--0.97, rescaled to {[}0, 1{]}).

Semantic repeatability and reproducibility of GPT-5 and Gemini 3 Flash on H5N1 One Health questions

Authors: Luizza Dalla Pria, Amelia L. Nelson, renata ivanek
Original abstract (English)
Highly pathogenic avian influenza A(H5N1) has re-emerged as a One Health concern since 2024, and the public is increasingly consulting large-language-model (LLM) chatbots for health information during outbreaks. Before a chatbot's accuracy can be meaningfully assessed, its consistency must first be understood, so that any single response can be trusted as a fair representative of the model's behavior. We characterized the semantic repeatability and reproducibility of GPT-5 (OpenAI) and Gemini 3 Flash (Google) on 15 H5N1 One Health questions sourced from social media discourse by adopting and extending a centroid-based evaluation framework. Each question was submitted 30 times and reformulated using five Chain-of-Thought (CoT) variants with 30 runs each, yielding 5,400 responses. Pairwise cosine similarity was computed on MPNet embeddings, with sensitivity analyses across three additional transformers. Statistical inference combined standard nonparametric tests (paired Wilcoxon and Friedman with Holm--Bonferroni correction) with inter-embedding agreement statistics (ICC(2,k) and Cronbach's $\alpha$) introduced here as a methodological addition. Both models produced high mean consistency (0.93--0.97, rescaled to {[}0, 1{]}). GPT-5 showed reproducibility significantly higher than repeatability (W = 3.0, p = 0.0003), whereas for Gemini 3 Flash the two metrics were statistically indistinguishable (W = 42.0, p = 0.33). Differential Diagnosis ranked among the top two CoT framings for consistency in both models. The tested chatbots behave as semantically stable sources on H5N1 One Health questions, and the developed consistency evaluation framework is portable to other domains.

Highly pathogenic avian influenza A(H5N1) has re-emerged as a One Health concern since 2024, and the public is increasingly consulting large-language-model (LLM) chatbots for health information during outbreaks.

Main results

Interpretation and novelty

利用逆转录环介导的等温扩增,开发一种灵敏、快速的野生动物 禽流感 病毒现场检测方法。

Development of a sensitive and rapid on-site detection method for avian influenza virus in wildlife using reverse transcription loop-mediated isothermal amplification.
作者： Tatsuru Morita, Yo Shimazu, Takahiro Hiono, Tomoki Kawase, Fuminori Maki, Norikazu Isoda, Mitsuaki Okami, Masaki Izawa, Yoshihiro Sakoda
摘要中文翻译H5高致病性禽流感病毒(HPAIV)的全球传播严重威胁野生动物,对保护医学提出严峻挑战。尽管免疫色谱 (IC) 试剂盒通常用于野生动物样本的现场 诊断,但其低灵敏度常常会导致假阴性。高度灵敏的分子方法,例如逆转录定量聚合酶链式反应 (RT-qPCR),由于需要复杂的设备,通常在现场使用时不切实际。因此,灵敏且经济实惠的现场诊断工具对于在野外采集的野生动物样本中早期检测 HPAIV 至关重要。在本研究中,开发并评估了一种针对 M 基因的逆转录环介导等温扩增 (RT-LAMP) 检测方法,用于现场检测 禽流感 病毒 (AIV)。最初,使用液体试剂进行的测定在特异性和灵敏度方面进行了验证,以确认反应混合物组成的适当性。该测定表现出高特异性,并且专门检测 AIV,且无交叉反应。使用 HPAIV 感染鸭的拭子进行的敏感性分析表明,该检测方法与 RT-qPCR 相当,但明显优于 IC 试剂盒。反应试剂被冻干,以最大限度地减少实际现场使用的测试程序。这种使用冻干试剂和便携式培养箱的简化方案产生的结果与 RT-qPCR 的结果高度一致。这些发现表明,RT-LAMP 检测可作为一种强大的诊断资源,通过在现场快速、灵敏和早期检测 AIV 感染来支持野生动物保护策略。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决在野生动物中检测 禽流感 病毒的严峻挑战,因为 H5 高致病性 禽流感 病毒 (HPAIV) 在全球的传播严重威胁野生动物和保护医学。
设计与对象
本研究开发并评估了一种逆转录环介导等温扩增 (RT-LAMP) 测定法,用于现场检测野生动物样本中的 禽流感 病毒 (AIV)。该研究是在现场进行的,重点是感染 HPAIV 的鸭子。
核心方法
开发并验证了针对 M 基因的逆转录环介导等温扩增 (RT-LAMP) 检测,用于 禽流感 病毒 (AIV) 的现场检测。使用感染 HPAIV 的鸭子的拭子对该测定进行测试,以评估其敏感性和特异性。
主要结果
RT-LAMP 检测显示出高特异性,并且与其他病毒没有交叉反应。敏感性分析表明,它与 RT-qPCR 相当,但明显比免疫层析 (IC) 试剂盒更敏感。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,RT-LAMP 检测是一种强大的诊断工具,可在现场快速、灵敏地检测 禽流感 病毒 (AIV),为传统实验室方法提供了实用的替代方案。
科研与公卫意义
RT-LAMP 检测可以通过在现场早期、准确地检测 禽流感 病毒 (AIV) 来支持野生动物保护策略,这对于监测和预防 HPAIV 的传播至关重要。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于对感染 HPAIV 的鸭子进行检测,证据仅基于抽象数据,可能无法完全体现在不同现场条件下的实际应用。证据强度中等。

Development of a sensitive and rapid on-site detection method for avian influenza virus in wildlife using reverse transcription loop-mediated isothermal amplification.

Authors: Tatsuru Morita, Yo Shimazu, Takahiro Hiono, Tomoki Kawase, Fuminori Maki, Norikazu Isoda, Mitsuaki Okami, Masaki Izawa, Yoshihiro Sakoda
Original abstract (English)
The global spread of H5 high pathogenicity avian influenza virus (HPAIV) severely threatens wildlife, posing a critical challenge for conservation medicine. Although immunochromatographic (IC) kits are commonly used for on-site diagnosis of wildlife samples, their low sensitivity often results in false negatives. Highly sensitive molecular methods, such as reverse transcription quantitative polymerase chain reaction (RT-qPCR), are often impractical for field use because of the need for sophisticated equipment. Therefore, a sensitive and affordable on-site diagnostic tool is essential for early detection of HPAIV in wildlife samples collected in the field. In this study, a reverse transcription loop-mediated isothermal amplification (RT-LAMP) assay targeting M gene for the on-site detection of avian influenza virus (AIV) was developed and evaluated. Initially, the assay using liquid reagents was validated in terms of specificity and sensitivity to confirm the appropriateness of the reaction mixture composition. This assay exhibited high specificity and exclusively detected AIVs without cross-reactivity. Sensitivity analysis using swabs from HPAIV-infected ducks demonstrated that the assay was comparable to RT-qPCR but substantially superior to IC kits. The reaction reagents were lyophilized to minimize the testing procedure for practical field use. This simplified protocol using lyophilized reagents and a portable incubator yielded results that were highly concordant with those of RT-qPCR. These findings indicate that the RT-LAMP assay serves as a robust diagnostic resource, supporting wildlife conservation strategies by enabling rapid, sensitive, and early detection of AIV infection in the field.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the critical challenge of detecting avian influenza virus in wildlife, as the global spread of H5 high pathogenicity avian influenza virus (HPAIV) severely threatens wildlife and conservation medicine.
Design and population
This study developed and evaluated a reverse transcription loop-mediated isothermal amplification (RT-LAMP) assay for on-site detection of avian influenza virus (AIV) in wildlife samples. The study was conducted in the field, with the focus on HPAIV-infected ducks.
Core methods
A reverse transcription loop-mediated isothermal amplification (RT-LAMP) assay targeting the M gene was developed and validated for on-site detection of avian influenza virus (AIV). The assay was tested using swabs from HPAIV-infected ducks to assess its sensitivity and specificity.
Main results
The RT-LAMP assay demonstrated high specificity and no cross-reactivity with other viruses. Sensitivity analysis showed that it was comparable to RT-qPCR but significantly more sensitive than immunochromatographic (IC) kits.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings indicate that the RT-LAMP assay is a robust diagnostic tool for the rapid and sensitive detection of avian influenza virus (AIV) in the field, offering a practical alternative to traditional laboratory-based methods.
Scientific and public-health significance
The RT-LAMP assay can support wildlife conservation strategies by enabling early and accurate detection of avian influenza virus (AIV) in the field, which is crucial for monitoring and preventing the spread of HPAIV.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was limited to testing the assay on HPAIV-infected ducks, and the evidence is based on abstract-only data, which may not fully capture the practical application in diverse field conditions. The evidence strength is moderate.

人 H5N1 A 血凝素 E190D 和 Q226H 微小变异的结构和功能表征/不列颠哥伦比亚省/PHL-2032/2024

Structural and functional characterization of hemagglutinin E190D and Q226H minor variants in human H5N1 A/British Columbia/PHL-2032/2024
作者： Ting-Hui Lin, Xue-Yong Zhu, Jefferson José da Silva Santos, Sheng-Yang Wang, Simeon Babarinde, Wen-Li Yu, Ryan McBride, James C. Paulson, Scott E. Hensley, Ian A. Wilson
摘要中文翻译2024 年末,从不列颠哥伦比亚省的严重人类感染中分离出源自进化枝 2.3.4.4b 的禽 H5N1 流感病毒 (A/British Columbia/PHL-2032/2024),揭示了 E190D 和 Q226H 替换的罕见组合,作为受体结合位点 (RBS) 中的微小变异。我们重组产生了含有这些 突变 的血凝素 (HA),并进行了受体结合和结构分析。具有标志残基 E190 和 Q226 的野生型 HA 保留了与 α2-3 N 连接聚糖的强结合,而 E190D 和 Q226H 消除了与 α2-3 N 连接聚糖和 O 连接聚糖的结合,并且没有转换为 α2-6 唾液酸苷。体积较大的 H226 引起 RBS 内的空间冲突,而 E190D 消除了与 Sia-1 的保守氢键,破坏了受体的结合。尽管该分离株中的 D190 和 H226 不利于与已知聚糖受体的结合,并且与人与人之间的 传播 不相容,但持续的 传播 对于监测循环 H5N1 病毒中关键 RBS 残基的任何变化至关重要。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在调查从不列颠哥伦比亚省严重人类感染中分离出的禽 H5N1 流感病毒的受体结合位点 (RBS) 中的微小变异 E190D 和 Q226H 的影响,因为这些替换被认为是罕见的,并且对病毒功能可能具有重要意义。
设计与对象
该研究涉及使用 E190D 和 Q226H 突变 重组生产血凝素 (HA),并对这些变体进行受体结合和结构分析,将它们与具有标志残基 E190 和 Q226 的野生型 HA 进行比较。
核心方法
研究人员用 E190D 和 Q226H 突变 生产了重组血凝素 (HA),并进行了受体结合测定和结构分析,以评估它们与聚糖受体的相互作用。
主要结果
野生型 HA 保留了与 α2-3 N 连接聚糖的强结合,而 E190D 和 Q226H 变体消除了与 α2-3 N 连接聚糖和 O 连接聚糖的结合,并且没有转换为 α2-6 唾液酸苷。Q226H 突变 引起 RBS 内的空间冲突,E190D 突变 消除了与 Sia-1 的保守氢键,破坏了受体的结合。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,该 H5N1 分离株的 RBS 中的 E190D 和 Q226H 取代不利于受体结合,这可能会阻碍其与人类受体结合的能力,尽管持续的 监测 对于监测循环病毒的潜在变化是必要的。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果凸显了监测 H5N1 病毒中关键受体结合位点 (RBS) 残基以评估其对人类的适应潜力和 传播 的重要性,为 监测 和公共卫生策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于重组血凝素的结构和功能分析,证据基于体外实验,可能无法完全代表体内行为。证据强度中等。

Structural and functional characterization of hemagglutinin E190D and Q226H minor variants in human H5N1 A/British Columbia/PHL-2032/2024

Authors: Ting-Hui Lin, Xue-Yong Zhu, Jefferson José da Silva Santos, Sheng-Yang Wang, Simeon Babarinde, Wen-Li Yu, Ryan McBride, James C. Paulson, Scott E. Hensley, Ian A. Wilson
Original abstract (English)
In late 2024, an avian H5N1 influenza virus derived from clade 2.3.4.4b was isolated from a severe human infection in British Columbia (A/British Columbia/PHL-2032/2024), revealing a rare combination of substitutions E190D and Q226H as minor variants in the receptor-binding site (RBS). We recombinantly produced hemagglutinins (HA) harboring these mutations and conducted receptor-binding and structural analyses. The wild-type HA with hallmark residues E190 and Q226 retained strong binding to α2-3 N-linked glycans, whereas E190D and Q226H abolished binding to α2-3 N- and O-linked glycans and no switch to α2-6 sialosides. The bulkier H226 caused steric clashes within the RBS, whereas E190D eliminated a conserved hydrogen bond with Sia-1, disrupting receptor engagement. Although D190 and H226 in this isolate are deleterious to engagement with known glycan receptors and not compatible with human-to-human transmission, continued surveillance is essential to monitor any changes in key RBS residues in circulating H5N1 viruses.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to investigate the impact of minor variants E190D and Q226H in the receptor-binding site (RBS) of an avian H5N1 influenza virus isolated from a severe human infection in British Columbia, as these substitutions were identified as rare and potentially significant for viral function.
Design and population
The study involved recombinant production of hemagglutinins (HA) with the E190D and Q226H mutations and conducted receptor-binding and structural analyses on these variants, comparing them to the wild-type HA with hallmark residues E190 and Q226.
Core methods
The researchers produced recombinant hemagglutinins (HA) with the E190D and Q226H mutations and performed receptor-binding assays and structural analyses to evaluate their interactions with glycan receptors.
Main results
The wild-type HA retained strong binding to α2-3 N-linked glycans, while the E190D and Q226H variants abolished binding to both α2-3 N- and O-linked glycans and did not switch to α2-6 sialosides. The Q226H mutation caused steric clashes within the RBS, and the E190D mutation eliminated a conserved hydrogen bond with Sia-1, disrupting receptor engagement.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings demonstrate that the E190D and Q226H substitutions in the RBS of this H5N1 isolate are detrimental to receptor binding, which may hinder its ability to engage with human receptors, though continued surveillance is necessary to monitor potential changes in circulating viruses.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results highlight the importance of monitoring key receptor-binding site (RBS) residues in H5N1 viruses to assess their potential for adaptation and transmission to humans, informing surveillance and public health strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to structural and functional analyses of recombinant hemagglutinins, and the evidence is based on in vitro experiments, which may not fully represent in vivo behavior. The evidence strength is moderate.

用于奶牛、鸡和人类甲型流感 H5 抗体和 NP 抗原检测的物种不可知工具

Species-agnostic tools for Influenza A H5 antibody and NP antigen detection in dairy cows, chickens, and humans
作者： Laura R. H. Ahlers, Nicholas Sammons, Brian S. Lane, Ye-Ming Wang, Leonid Dzantiev, Anu Mathew, Jason Abraham, Ismaila Ademola Shittu, Lyudmyla V. Marushchak, Gregory C. Gray
摘要中文翻译高致病性 禽流感 (HPAI) A (H5N1) 在 野生鸟类 中传播,在家禽中引起 暴发,但最近在美国成为地方性流行病,并蔓延到包括牛在内的各种哺乳动物中。H5N1 进化枝 2.3.4.4b 的人畜共患潜力强调了在哺乳动物和鸟类物种中需要 监测 工具。我们使用 Meso Scale Discovery (MSD) 平台开发了两种与物种无关的测定法,以增强当前的 监测 方法:针对甲型流感核蛋白 (NP) 抗原的夹心免疫测定法,以及针对甲型流感/H5 特异性抗体的桥接 血清学 测定法。我们通过测试来自经历 H5N1 入侵的牛群的牛奶和血清、来自接种 H5N1 疫苗的人类和鸡的血清以及来自牛、鸡和人类的阴性对照样本来评估检测结果。在暴露于 H5 的牛群的牛奶和血清中检测到了抗体和抗原,但在假定的未接触牛群的样本中未检测到抗体和抗原。通过结合抗原和抗体测试,增加了确定的暴露动物的数量。相对于未接种 H5 的对照,桥接 血清学 测定可准确地区分 H5 疫苗接种鸡和人的血清。使用 H1 血凝素封闭可减少潜在的交叉反应,从而改善了 H5 疫苗接种者样本和假定阴性人类样本之间的区分。这些研究证明了这些方法对不同物种的适用性,并促进它们在 监测 研究中的使用。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在满足针对 H5N1 禽流感 改进 监测 工具的需求,这种病毒已在美国流行,并蔓延到包括牛在内的各种哺乳动物中。
设计与对象
该研究涉及测试来自经历 H5N1 入侵的牛群的牛奶和血清、来自接种 H5N1 疫苗的人类和鸡的血清,以及来自牛、鸡和人类的阴性对照样本。
核心方法
使用 Meso Scale Discovery (MSD) 平台开发了两种与物种无关的测定法:针对甲型流感核蛋白 (NP) 抗原的夹心免疫测定法和针对甲型流感/H5 特异性抗体的桥接 血清学 测定法。使用 H1 血凝素封闭可提高 H5 疫苗接种者样本与假定阴性人类样本之间的区分度。
主要结果
在暴露于 H5 的牛群的牛奶和血清中检测到了抗体和抗原,但在假定的未接触牛群的样本中未检测到抗体和抗原。结合抗原和抗体测试增加了已识别的暴露动物的数量。
解释与创新
这些检测方法证明了检测多个物种的 H5N1 暴露的有效性,突出了它们在增强 监测 的 禽流感 工作方面的效用。
科研与公卫意义
这些方法可以改进不同物种中 H5N1 的检测,有助于 监测 及其人畜共患传播。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于抽象证据,结果基于单一测试方法,表明对研究结果有一定的信心。

Species-agnostic tools for Influenza A H5 antibody and NP antigen detection in dairy cows, chickens, and humans

Authors: Laura R. H. Ahlers, Nicholas Sammons, Brian S. Lane, Ye-Ming Wang, Leonid Dzantiev, Anu Mathew, Jason Abraham, Ismaila Ademola Shittu, Lyudmyla V. Marushchak, Gregory C. Gray
Original abstract (English)
High Pathogenicity Avian Influenza (HPAI) A (H5N1) circulates in wild birds, causing outbreaks in domestic poultry, but recently became enzootic in the United States spilling over into various mammals, including cattle. The zoonotic potential of H5N1 clade 2.3.4.4b emphasizes the need for tools for surveillance in mammalian and avian species. We developed two species-agnostic assays using the Meso Scale Discovery (MSD) platform to enhance current surveillance methods: a sandwich immunoassay for influenza A nucleoprotein (NP) antigen, and a bridging serology assay for influenza A/H5 specific antibodies. We assessed the assays by testing milk and serum from cattle herds experiencing H5N1 incursions, serum from H5N1-vaccinated humans and chickens, and negative control samples from cattle, chickens, and humans. Antibodies and antigen were detected in milk and serum from H5-exposed cattle herds, but not in samples from putatively naïve herds. The number of identified exposed animals increased by combining antigen and antibody testing. The bridging serology assay accurately differentiated serum from H5-vaccinated chickens and humans, relative to H5-naïve controls. Blocking with H1 hemagglutinin improved discrimination between H5-vaccinated and presumed-negative human samples by reducing potential cross-reactivity. The studies demonstrate the applicability of these methods to diverse species and facilitate their use in surveillance studies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the need for improved surveillance tools for H5N1 Avian Influenza, which has become enzootic in the United States and spills over into various mammals, including cattle.
Design and population
The study involved testing milk and serum from cattle herds experiencing H5N1 incursions, serum from H5N1-vaccinated humans and chickens, and negative control samples from cattle, chickens, and humans.
Core methods
Two species-agnostic assays were developed using the Meso Scale Discovery (MSD) platform: a sandwich immunoassay for influenza A nucleoprotein (NP) antigen and a bridging serology assay for influenza A/H5 specific antibodies. Blocking with H1 hemagglutinin was used to improve discrimination between H5-vaccinated and presumed-negative human samples.
Main results
Antibodies and antigen were detected in milk and serum from H5-exposed cattle herds, but not in samples from putatively naïve herds. Combining antigen and antibody testing increased the number of identified exposed animals.
Interpretation and novelty
The assays demonstrated effectiveness in detecting H5N1 exposure across multiple species, highlighting their utility in enhancing surveillance efforts for avian influenza.
Scientific and public-health significance
These methods can improve the detection of H5N1 in diverse species, aiding in the surveillance of avian influenza and its zoonotic spread.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was limited to abstract-only evidence, and the results are based on a single testing approach, suggesting moderate confidence in the findings.

欧洲 疫苗 中心

The European Vaccines Hub
作者： Rino Rappuoli, Isabelle Bekeredjian‐Ding, Pierre Van Damme, James P. Di Santo, Donata Medaglini, Michel Pletschette, Rappuoli R, Bekeredjian-Ding I, Van Damme P, Di Santo JP
摘要中文翻译EVH成立一年后,四大支柱均启动了多项项目并取得了重要成果。Pillar 3 已设计并全面招募了一项临床试验,以研究欧洲人群对 禽流感 H5N1 初次免疫和加强免疫的 疫苗 反应,目前正在评估其结果。该研究还有额外的范围来建立一项研究 队列研究,其外周血细胞可用于发现广泛的中和抗体,这些抗体可识别 2006 年和 2026 年 H5N1 毒株之间共享的表位。自然免疫学第 27 卷 | 2026 年 12 月 | 2363–2365 | 2363 欧洲 疫苗 中心 Rino Rappuoli、Isabelle Bekeredjian-Ding、Pierre Van Damme、James P. Di Santo、Donata Medaglini 和 Michel Pletschette 欧洲 疫苗 中心是一个新颖的设计联盟,汇集了参与 疫苗 开发的欧洲领先组织。其使命是开发 疫苗 和单克隆抗体,确保欧洲应对流行病和新出现的感染做好准备。当 COVID-19 大流行袭击欧盟时,欧盟没有做好充分准备来应对毁灭性的健康和经济紧急情况。尽管欧洲生物技术公司通过 Comirnaty 等产品为 疫苗 的开发做出了巨大贡献,但德国 BioNTech 开发的 mRNA 疫苗 最终被美国公司授权并商业化,这意味着在疫情高峰期,欧洲医疗对策很少进入市场。发现的另一个弱点是欧洲没有参与任何导致获得许可的关键疗效试验。为了解决这种情况,欧盟委员会成立了卫生应急准备和响应局(HERA)作为其主管机构之一...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决欧盟对流行病和新出现的感染缺乏准备的问题,特别强调需要一个协调的系统来开发和提供 疫苗 和针对具有流行和大流行潜力的优先病原体的单克隆抗体。
设计与对象
该研究描述了欧洲 疫苗 中心 (EVH) 的建立,作为由成员国资助的现有组织网络,旨在为整个欧洲的 疫苗 开发、临床试验和制造准备创建一个敏捷系统。
核心方法
EVH 采用公私合作模式,通过临时 1 期和 2 期研究协调临床开发,并为大规模 3 期试验做好准备。它还绘制了 疫苗 制造的欧洲 GMP 设施图,并开发了在大流行期间快速扩展的概念。
主要结果
EVH 进行了一项临床试验,以评估欧洲人群对 禽流感 H5N1 初次免疫和加强免疫的 疫苗 反应,目前正在评估其结果。此外,它还发现了一种有效的人类单克隆抗体,可以中和扎伊尔埃博拉病毒株和本迪布焦埃博拉病毒株。
解释与创新
作者解释说,EVH 已经证明了通过利用欧洲实体原型的临时开发来提供创新对策的潜力,展示了其在大流行病防范方面的敏捷性和有效性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果为欧洲大流行病应对系统的发展提供了信息,强调了 疫苗 开发、临床试验和制造协调网络的重要性,该网络可应用于其他具有流行潜力的优先病原体。
局限与证据强度
证据基于 EVH 的设计和初始活动,由于缺乏详细结果以及依赖计划研究而不是已完成的研究,因此具有中等置信度。

The European Vaccines Hub

Authors: Rino Rappuoli, Isabelle Bekeredjian‐Ding, Pierre Van Damme, James P. Di Santo, Donata Medaglini, Michel Pletschette, Rappuoli R, Bekeredjian-Ding I, Van Damme P, Di Santo JP
Original abstract (English)
One year after the EVH was established, each of the four pillars has initiated a variety of projects and delivered important results. Pillar 3 has designed and fully enrolled a clinical trial to study the vaccine response of the European population to primary and booster immunization against the avian influenza H5N1, and is currently assessing its outcome. The study has the additional scope to establish a study cohort whose peripheral blood cells can be used to discover broadly neutralizing antibodies that recognize epitopes shared between the 2006 and 2026 H5N1 strains. nature immunology Volume 27 | December 2026 | 2363–2365 | 2363 The European Vaccines Hub Rino Rappuoli, Isabelle Bekeredjian-Ding, Pierre Van Damme, James P. Di Santo, Donata Medaglini & Michel Pletschette The European Vaccines Hub is a novel design consortium that assembles leading European organizations involved in vaccine development. The mission is to develop vaccines and monoclonal antibodies ensuring Europe’s pandemics and emerging infections readiness. When the COVID-19 pandemic struck the European Union, it was insufficiently prepared to face the devastating health and economic emergency. Although European biotech contributed significantly to the development of vaccines with products such as Comirnaty, the mRNA vaccine developed by the German BioNTech was eventually licensed to and commercialized by an American company, meaning that very few European medical countermeasures reached the market at the peak of the pandemic. A further weakness identified was that Europe did not partici- pate in any of the pivotal efficacy trials leading to licensure. To address this situation, the European Commission established the Health Emergency Preparedness and Response Authority (HERA) as one of its Directora...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the European Union’s lack of preparedness for pandemics and emerging infections, particularly highlighting the need for a coordinated system to develop and deliver vaccines and monoclonal antibodies against priority pathogens with epidemic and pandemic potential.
Design and population
The study describes the establishment of the European Vaccines Hub (EVH) as a network of existing organizations funded by member states, designed to create an agile system for vaccine development, clinical trials, and manufacturing readiness across Europe.
Core methods
The EVH employs a public–private partnership model, coordinates clinical development through ad hoc phase 1 and 2 studies, and prepares for large-scale phase 3 trials. It also maps European GMP facilities for vaccine manufacturing and develops concepts for rapid scaling during pandemics.
Main results
The EVH has conducted a clinical trial to assess the vaccine response of the European population to primary and booster immunization against avian influenza H5N1 and is currently evaluating its outcomes. Additionally, it has identified a potent human monoclonal antibody that neutralizes both Ebola Zaire and Ebola Bundibugyo strains.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that the EVH has demonstrated the potential to deliver innovative countermeasures by leveraging ad hoc development of prototypes from European entities, showcasing its agility and effectiveness in pandemic preparedness.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the development of a resilient European pandemic preparedness system, emphasizing the importance of a coordinated network for vaccine development, clinical trials, and manufacturing, which can be applied to other priority pathogens with epidemic potential.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is based on the design and initial activities of the EVH, with a moderate confidence level due to the absence of detailed results and the reliance on planned rather than completed studies.
