流感 mRNA 疫苗 脂质纳米颗粒的比较评价:免疫原性和交叉反应性 免疫应答

Comparative Evaluation of Lipid Nanoparticles for Influenza mRNA Vaccines: Immunogenicity and Cross-Reactive Immune Responses
作者： Yuanxin Sun
摘要中文翻译由于季节性流行、持续抗原 进化 以及 高致病性禽流感 病毒的潜在出现,流感仍然是全球重大公共卫生问题。信使 RNA (mRNA) 疫苗 为 疫苗 的快速开发提供灵活的平台;然而,脂质纳米颗粒 (LNP) 制剂对 疫苗 诱导的免疫原性和交叉保护反应的影响仍不清楚。这项临床前研究旨在比较用三种不同 LNP 配制的流感 mRNA 疫苗 的免疫原性和保护功效:ALC-0315、SM-102 和新型 SX-104 制剂。使用 BALB/c 小鼠模型,将评估编码来自季节性流感 A(H1N1)、A(H3N2)、B/Victoria 和 B/Yamagata 毒株的血凝素 (HA) 抗原的四价 mRNA 疫苗。预先存在的免疫力对 疫苗 反应的影响也将被研究。体液 免疫应答 将使用微中和测定进行评估,而细胞 免疫应答 将通过细胞内细胞因子染色和流式细胞术进行表征,以评估抗原特异性 CD4+ 和 CD8+ T 细胞反应。将针对抗原上不同的 H1N1 和 H3N2 毒株研究交叉反应免疫,包括针对保守 HA 区域的 T 细胞反应。此外,动物攻击研究将评估针对流感感染的保护功效,并研究体液和细胞免疫对 疫苗 介导的保护的贡献。还将评估针对 高致病性禽流感 H5 和 H7 病毒的潜在交叉保护作用。研究结果预计将深入了解不同的 LNP 制剂如何影响流感 疫苗 诱导的 免疫应答 的程度和广度,支持 mRNA 疫苗 制剂的优化和更广泛的流感 疫苗接种 策略的开发。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
由于季节性流行、持续抗原 进化 以及 高致病性禽流感 病毒的潜在出现,流感仍然是全球重大公共卫生问题。该研究旨在解决对脂质纳米颗粒 (LNP) 制剂如何影响 疫苗 诱导的免疫原性和交叉保护反应的认识不足。
设计与对象
这项临床前研究比较了用三种不同的 LNP 配制的流感 mRNA 疫苗 的免疫原性和保护功效:ALC-0315、SM-102 和新型 SX-104 制剂。使用 BALB/c 小鼠模型,将评估编码来自季节性流感 A(H1N1)、A(H3N2)、B/Victoria 和 B/Yamagata 毒株的血凝素 (HA) 抗原的四价 mRNA 疫苗。
核心方法
体液 免疫应答 将使用微中和测定进行评估,而细胞 免疫应答 将通过细胞内细胞因子染色和流式细胞术进行表征,以评估抗原特异性 CD4+ 和 CD8+ T 细胞反应。将针对抗原上不同的 H1N1 和 H3N2 毒株研究交叉反应免疫,包括针对保守 HA 区域的 T 细胞反应。
主要结果
预先存在的免疫力对 疫苗 反应的影响也将被研究。动物挑战研究将评估针对流感感染的保护功效,并研究体液和细胞免疫对 疫苗 介导的保护的贡献。还将评估针对 高致病性禽流感 H5 和 H7 病毒的潜在交叉保护作用。
解释与创新
研究结果预计将深入了解不同的 LNP 制剂如何影响流感 疫苗 诱导的 免疫应答 的程度和广度,支持 mRNA 疫苗 制剂的优化和更广泛的流感 疫苗接种 策略的开发。
科研与公卫意义
该研究可以为针对 禽流感 A 病毒开发更有效的 mRNA 疫苗 制剂提供信息,从而有可能增强针对新兴病毒株的交叉保护性免疫力,并改善针对动物和人类群体的 疫苗接种 策略。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据未报告具体限制或证据强度评估。

Comparative Evaluation of Lipid Nanoparticles for Influenza mRNA Vaccines: Immunogenicity and Cross-Reactive Immune Responses

Authors: Yuanxin Sun
Original abstract (English)
Influenza remains a significant global public health concern due to seasonal epidemics, continuous antigenic evolution, and the potential emergence of highly pathogenic avian influenza viruses. Messenger RNA (mRNA) vaccines offer a flexible platform for rapid vaccine development; however, the influence of lipid nanoparticle (LNP) formulations on vaccine-induced immunogenicity and cross-protective responses remains insufficiently understood. This preclinical study aims to compare the immunogenicity and protective efficacy of influenza mRNA vaccines formulated with three different LNPs: ALC-0315, SM-102, and the novel SX-104 formulation. Using BALB/c mouse models, quadrivalent mRNA vaccines encoding haemagglutinin (HA) antigens from seasonal influenza A(H1N1), A(H3N2), B/Victoria, and B/Yamagata strains will be evaluated. The impact of pre-existing immunity on vaccine responses will also be investigated. Humoral immune responses will be assessed using microneutralization assays, while cellular immune responses will be characterized through intracellular cytokine staining and flow cytometry to evaluate antigen-specific CD4+ and CD8+ T-cell responses. Cross-reactive immunity will be investigated against antigenically distinct H1N1 and H3N2 strains, including T-cell responses targeting conserved HA regions. Furthermore, animal challenge studies will assess protective efficacy against influenza infection and investigate the contributions of humoral and cellular immunity to vaccine-mediated protection. The potential cross-protective effects against highly pathogenic avian influenza H5 and H7 viruses will also be evaluated. The findings are expected to provide insights into how different LNP formulations influence the magnitude and breadth of influenza vaccine-induced immune responses, supporting the optimization of mRNA vaccine formulations and the development of broader influenza vaccination strategies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Influenza remains a significant global public health concern due to seasonal epidemics, continuous antigenic evolution, and the potential emergence of highly pathogenic avian influenza viruses. The study aims to address the insufficient understanding of how lipid nanoparticle (LNP) formulations influence vaccine-induced immunogenicity and cross-protective responses.
Design and population
This preclinical study compares the immunogenicity and protective efficacy of influenza mRNA vaccines formulated with three different LNPs: ALC-0315, SM-102, and the novel SX-104 formulation. Using BALB/c mouse models, quadrivalent mRNA vaccines encoding haemagglutinin (HA) antigens from seasonal influenza A(H1N1), A(H3N2), B/Victoria, and B/Yamagata strains will be evaluated.
Core methods
Humoral immune responses will be assessed using microneutralization assays, while cellular immune responses will be characterized through intracellular cytokine staining and flow cytometry to evaluate antigen-specific CD4+ and CD8+ T-cell responses. Cross-reactive immunity will be investigated against antigenically distinct H1N1 and H3N2 strains, including T-cell responses targeting conserved HA regions.
Main results
The impact of pre-existing immunity on vaccine responses will also be investigated. Animal challenge studies will assess protective efficacy against influenza infection and investigate the contributions of humoral and cellular immunity to vaccine-mediated protection. The potential cross-protective effects against highly pathogenic avian influenza H5 and H7 viruses will also be evaluated.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings are expected to provide insights into how different LNP formulations influence the magnitude and breadth of influenza vaccine-induced immune responses, supporting the optimization of mRNA vaccine formulations and the development of broader influenza vaccination strategies.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study can inform the development of more effective mRNA vaccine formulations for avian influenza A viruses, potentially enhancing cross-protective immunity against emerging strains and improving vaccination strategies for both animal and human populations.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations or evidence strength assessment.

Prioritization of Influenza Type A Virus Genomes as Possible Recombinants

Authors: Tommaso Alfonsi, Anna Bernasconi, Matteo Chiara, Stefano Ceri
Original abstract (English)
In this work, we revisit our RecombinHunt method, previously applied extensively to the SARS-CoV-2 pandemic, and extend its use to Influenza A viruses, focusing on the H1N1 and H5N1 genotypes. https://doi.org/10.24976/Discov.Med.202537196.70 - Focosi, D., Maggi, F., Franchini, M., McConnell, S., Casadevall, A.: Analysis of immune escape variants from antibody-based therapeutics against COVID-19: a systematic review. https://doi.org/10.3390/life16050776 - Pinoli, P., et al.: Varianthunter: a method and tool for fast detection of emerging sars-cov-2 variants. Computational Intelligence Methods for Bioinformatics and Biostatistics. Abstract Recombination plays a central role in viral evolution, and the increasing volume of publicly available genomic data offers an unprecedented opportunity to investigate these events across diverse pathogens. With the rapid expansion of genomic surveillance, efficient and automated analysis pipelines have become essential. Although intra-segment recombination in influenza is generally considered unlikely and is often regarded as biologically negligible, the question has remained difficult to assess systematically. Here, leveraging the breadth of available influenza genomic data, we evaluate at scale whether any detectable signals of intra-segment recombination can be identified in hemagglutinin sequences. Our analysis prioritizes potential intra-segment recombination events; however, their quantitative and biological significance in Influenza A remains to be determined. Question and background
The study aimed to investigate whether detectable signals of intra-segment recombination can be identified in hemagglutinin sequences of Influenza A viruses, particularly in light of the challenges in systematically assessing recombination in these viruses.
Design and population
The study utilized genomic data from Influenza A viruses, specifically focusing on the H1N1 and H5N1 genotypes, to evaluate recombination events. It was conducted using a data-driven approach and leveraged publicly available sequences from the GISAID Initiative.
Core methods
The researchers extended their RecombinHunt method, previously used for SARS-CoV-2, to analyze Influenza A virus genomes. They focused on hemagglutinin sequences and applied automated pipelines to detect potential recombination events.
Main results
The analysis identified potential intra-segment recombination events in Influenza A virus hemagglutinin sequences, though the quantitative and biological significance of these events remains to be determined.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret their findings as a first step in understanding recombination in Influenza A viruses, highlighting the need for further research to assess the significance of these events.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings contribute to the development of more efficient genomic surveillance tools for influenza, which can aid in early detection of emerging variants and improve public health preparedness.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's results are limited by the need for further validation to determine the biological and quantitative significance of the detected recombination events, and the evidence is considered moderate due to the exploratory nature of the analysis.

用于传染性流感病毒免提取病毒 RNA 检测的仿生膜融合门控水凝胶生物传感器

Bioinspired Membrane Fusion–Gated Hydrogel Biosensor for Extraction‐Free Viral RNA Detection of Infectious Influenza Virus
作者： Byeonggeol Mun, Chaewon Park, HanSol Kim, Jaewook Lim, Yeonwoo Jeong, Seung Jae Oh, Inhee Maeng, Eun‐Kyung Lim, Daesub Song, Seungjoo Haam
摘要中文翻译由人畜共患 跨物种溢出 驱动的新发传染病需要快速可靠的分子诊断来进行病原体识别和 监测。然而,传统的核酸诊断无法区分感染性病毒粒子和非活性RNA片段,限制了感染性解决的病毒识别。在这里,我们报告了一种仿生膜涂层水凝胶(Memgel)传感器,它能够实现流感病毒的免提取分子检测,同时区分具有融合能力的病毒粒子和非感染性病毒材料。该平台将细胞模拟支持的脂质双层与封装重组酶聚合酶扩增(RPA)试剂的聚乙二醇二丙烯酸酯(PEGDA)水凝胶基质集成在一起。在模拟内体的酸性条件下,血凝素介导的膜融合选择性地将病毒基因组RNA从感染性病毒粒子递送到水凝胶基质中,触发序列特异性扩增,同时抑制来自游离病毒RNA片段的信号。作为概念验证,对 Memgel 传感器在模拟鼻液和原料奶基质中检测甲型流感 (H9N2) 的情况进行了评估,实现了 10 1.23 TCID 50 mL - 1 的检测限,同时在不提取 RNA 的情况下保持了稳健的检测性能。与便携式荧光读数器相结合,该系统展示了与基于荧光的紧凑型读数器的兼容性,以供未来的护理点实施。这种融合门控传感策略为新兴病毒病原体的感染性解析分子诊断提供了基于材料的框架。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
由人畜共患 跨物种溢出 驱动的新发传染病需要快速可靠的分子诊断来进行病原体识别和 监测。
设计与对象
该研究引入了一种仿生膜涂层水凝胶(Memgel)传感器,设计用于免提取检测流感病毒,重点是区分感染性病毒粒子和非感染性病毒材料。
核心方法
该平台将细胞模拟支持的脂质双层与封装重组酶聚合酶扩增(RPA)试剂的聚乙二醇二丙烯酸酯(PEGDA)水凝胶基质集成在一起。
主要结果
Memgel 传感器针对模拟鼻液和原料奶基质中的甲型流感 (H9N2) 检测进行了评估,实现了 10 1.23 TCID 50 mL - 1 的检测限,同时在不提取 RNA 的情况下保持了稳健的检测性能。
解释与创新
融合门控传感策略为新兴病毒病原体的感染性解析分子诊断提供了基于材料的框架。
科研与公卫意义
该系统展示了与基于荧光的紧凑型读数器的兼容性,可用于未来的护理点实施,为快速可靠地检测不同样本基质中的传染性流感病毒提供了潜力。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告该研究的具体局限性。

Bioinspired Membrane Fusion–Gated Hydrogel Biosensor for Extraction‐Free Viral RNA Detection of Infectious Influenza Virus

Authors: Byeonggeol Mun, Chaewon Park, HanSol Kim, Jaewook Lim, Yeonwoo Jeong, Seung Jae Oh, Inhee Maeng, Eun‐Kyung Lim, Daesub Song, Seungjoo Haam
Original abstract (English)
Emerging infectious diseases driven by zoonotic spillover demand rapid and reliable molecular diagnostics for pathogen identification and surveillance. However, conventional nucleic acid diagnostics cannot distinguish infectious virions from nonviable RNA fragments, limiting infectivity-resolved virus identification. Here, we report a bioinspired membrane-coated hydrogel (Memgel) sensor that enables extraction-free molecular detection of influenza virus while distinguishing fusion-competent virions from noninfectious viral material. The platform integrates a cell-mimetic supported lipid bilayer with a polyethylene glycol diacrylate (PEGDA) hydrogel matrix encapsulating recombinase polymerase amplification (RPA) reagents. Under endosome-mimicking acidic conditions, hemagglutinin-mediated membrane fusion selectively delivers viral genomic RNA from infectious virions into the hydrogel matrix, triggering sequence-specific amplification while suppressing signals from free viral RNA fragments. As a proof of concept, the Memgel sensor was evaluated for the detection of influenza A (H9N2) in simulated nasal fluid and raw milk matrices, achieving a limit of detection of 10 1.23 TCID 50 mL - 1 while maintaining robust detection performance without RNA extraction. Coupled with a portable fluorescence reader, the system demonstrates compatibility with compact fluorescence-based readout for future point-of-care implementation. This fusion-gated sensing strategy provides a materials-based framework for infectivity-resolved molecular diagnostics of emerging viral pathogens.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Emerging infectious diseases driven by zoonotic spillover demand rapid and reliable molecular diagnostics for pathogen identification and surveillance.
Design and population
The study introduces a bioinspired membrane-coated hydrogel (Memgel) sensor designed for extraction-free detection of influenza virus, with a focus on distinguishing infectious virions from noninfectious viral material.
Core methods
The platform integrates a cell-mimetic supported lipid bilayer with a polyethylene glycol diacrylate (PEGDA) hydrogel matrix encapsulating recombinase polymerase amplification (RPA) reagents.
Main results
The Memgel sensor was evaluated for the detection of influenza A (H9N2) in simulated nasal fluid and raw milk matrices, achieving a limit of detection of 10 1.23 TCID 50 mL - 1 while maintaining robust detection performance without RNA extraction.
Interpretation and novelty
The fusion-gated sensing strategy provides a materials-based framework for infectivity-resolved molecular diagnostics of emerging viral pathogens.
Scientific and public-health significance
The system demonstrates compatibility with compact fluorescence-based readout for future point-of-care implementation, offering potential for rapid and reliable detection of infectious influenza virus in diverse sample matrices.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations of the study.

走到一起:病毒重组、重排和上位生存的坐标解卷积

Come together: Coordinate deconvolution of viral recombination, reassortment, and epistatic survival
作者： Sergei L. Kosakovsky Pond, Steven Weaver, Danielle Callan, Jordan D. Zehr, Alexander G. Lucaci, Anton Nekrutenko, Darren Patrick Martin
摘要中文翻译病毒亚型和重组检测支撑分类学管理、临床耐药性跟踪和实时 暴发 监测。社区命名系统——例如 HIV-1 循环重组形式 (CRF)、SARS-CoV-2 Pango 谱系和甲型流感基因型目录——已经创建了不可或缺的共享词汇表,但快速的档案增长和频繁的跨谱系嵌合现象现在引入了一个补充层,将跨病毒家族的速度、来源和统计严谨性统一起来。在这里,我们提出了 EGGMAN(精确基因组谱系和马赛克祖先命名法),这是一个统一的框架,它将根据有原则的非马赛克创始人基础(第 1 层)映射物理亲本坐标与已建立的流行克隆的统计保护分配(第 2 层)分开。通过结合无参考重组清理 (RhizAeon)、出现锚定的创始人生根 (ChronAeon) 和便携式病原体规范,一个引擎可以在笔记本电脑或浏览器中以每秒数千个序列处理连续的 基因组(HIV-1、SARS-CoV-2、野生动物 sarbecoviruses)和分段重配病毒(A 型流感),从而保留传统进化枝名称,同时赋予新兴嵌合体透明、第一天基于坐标的标识符。在公共 监测 档案中,EGGMAN 协调了 HIV-1 参考目录和谱系,协调了斯坦福 HIVDB 中超过 120,000 条临床 pol 记录,将新兴 CRF 和单系区域谱系与聚合重组热点分开,并在没有时间前瞻的情况下跟踪未注释的 H5Nx 重配体和 SARS-CoV-2 X* 波。最后,761 个 HIV-1 嵌合谱系的普查水平图谱揭示了一个两阶段选择性筛,将 宿主 内蛋白质折叠活力(gp120 和 Gag-CA 核心)与 宿主 间完整 3' 调控和包膜盒的保存分开。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决 禽流感 A 病毒的病毒亚型和重组检测的挑战,这对于分类学管理、临床耐药性跟踪和实时 暴发 监测 至关重要。
设计与对象
该研究引入了 EGGMAN,这是一个统一框架,旨在高速处理 基因组 A 病毒的连续 基因组 和分段重配体(包括 H5Nx),同时保留传统进化枝名称并为新兴嵌合体提供透明标识符。
核心方法
EGGMAN 结合了无参考重组清洗 (RhizAeon)、出现锚定的创始人生根 (ChronAeon) 和便携式病原体规范来分析 禽流感 A 病毒 基因组 和重配体,从而能够跨多个病毒家族进行高效、准确的处理。
主要结果
该框架在笔记本电脑或浏览器中以每秒数千个序列的速度成功处理了 禽流感 A 病毒 基因组 和重配病毒(包括 H5Nx),保留了传统进化枝名称,同时为新兴嵌合体分配透明的、基于坐标的标识符。
解释与创新
EGGMAN 提供了一种协调病毒重组、重配和上位生存的反卷积的新颖方法,为 禽流感 A 病毒分析提供了统一且统计上严格的方法。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以通过实现快速、准确的基因组分析而无需时间前瞻,为改进 监测、诊断 和 禽流感 A 病毒(特别是 H5Nx 重配病毒)的跟踪提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于抽象级别的证据,并且该框架的性能尚未通过广泛的经验数据进行验证,导致置信度评估适中。

Come together: Coordinate deconvolution of viral recombination, reassortment, and epistatic survival

Authors: Sergei L. Kosakovsky Pond, Steven Weaver, Danielle Callan, Jordan D. Zehr, Alexander G. Lucaci, Anton Nekrutenko, Darren Patrick Martin
Original abstract (English)
Viral subtyping and recombination detection underpin taxonomic curation, clinical resistance tracking, and real-time outbreak surveillance. Community nomenclature systems—such as HIV-1 circulating recombinant forms (CRFs), SARS-CoV-2 Pango lineages, and Influenza A genotype catalogs—have created indispensable shared vocabularies, yet rapid archive growth and frequent cross-lineage chimerism now invite a complementary layer that unites speed, provenance, and statistical rigor across viral families. Here, we present EGGMAN (Exact Genomic Genealogy and Mosaic Ancestry Nomenclature), a unified framework that separates mapping physical parental coordinates against a principled, non-mosaic founder basis (Tier 1) from statistically guarded assignment to established epidemic clones (Tier 2). By combining reference-free recombination scrubbing (RhizAeon), emergence-anchored founder rooting (ChronAeon), and portable pathogen specifications, one engine processes contiguous genomes (HIV-1, SARS-CoV-2, wildlife sarbecoviruses) and segmented reassortants (Influenza A) at thousands of sequences per second on a laptop or in a browser—preserving legacy clade names while giving emerging chimeras transparent, coordinate-based identifiers on Day 1. Across public surveillance archives, EGGMAN harmonizes the HIV-1 reference catalog and genealogy, reconciles >120,000 clinical pol records in Stanford HIVDB, separates emerging CRFs and a monophyletic regional lineage from convergent recombination hotspots, and tracks unannotated H5Nx reassortants and SARS-CoV-2 X* waves without temporal lookahead. Finally, census-level mapping across 761 HIV-1 mosaic lineages reveals a two-stage selective sieve separating within-host protein-folding viability (gp120 and Gag-CA cores) from between-host preservation of an intact 3' regulatory and envelope cassette.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the challenges of viral subtyping and recombination detection in avian influenza A viruses, which are critical for taxonomic curation, clinical resistance tracking, and real-time outbreak surveillance.
Design and population
The research introduced EGGMAN, a unified framework designed to process contiguous genomes and segmented reassortants of avian influenza A viruses, including H5Nx, at high speeds while preserving legacy clade names and providing transparent identifiers for emerging chimeras.
Core methods
EGGMAN combines reference-free recombination scrubbing (RhizAeon), emergence-anchored founder rooting (ChronAeon), and portable pathogen specifications to analyze avian influenza A virus genomes and reassortants, enabling efficient and accurate processing across multiple viral families.
Main results
The framework successfully processed avian influenza A virus genomes and reassortants, including H5Nx, at thousands of sequences per second on a laptop or in a browser, preserving legacy clade names while assigning transparent, coordinate-based identifiers to emerging chimeras.
Interpretation and novelty
EGGMAN provides a novel approach to coordinate deconvolution of viral recombination, reassortment, and epistatic survival, offering a unified and statistically rigorous method for avian influenza A virus analysis.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform improved surveillance, diagnosis, and tracking of avian influenza A viruses, particularly H5Nx reassortants, by enabling rapid and accurate genomic analysis without requiring temporal lookahead.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to abstract-level evidence, and the framework's performance has not been validated with extensive empirical data, resulting in a moderate confidence assessment.

我们终于能战胜流感了吗?

Are we finally getting on top of influenza?
作者： Gary S. Grohmann
摘要中文翻译季节性流感仍然是发病和 死亡率 的主要原因,特别是在老年人中,但随着美国食品和药物管理局批准 mRNA 疫苗 用于 50 岁以上人群的季节性流感,我们应该会看到病例和死亡人数显着减少。Moderna 的 mRNA-1010 已表现出优于标准剂量流感 疫苗 的功效,同时保持了可接受的安全性。该平台具有重要的优势,包括快速生产和更及时的 疫苗 菌株选择,这将改善针对抗原漂移的保护。mRNA 技术还可以实现针对流感、COVID-19 和其他呼吸道病毒的 疫苗 组合。与新兴的 H5N1 mRNA 疫苗 一起,这些进展可能会大大改善流感控制并加强对未来大流行威胁的准备。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了改善流感控制和对未来大流行威胁做好准备的需要,特别是考虑到 禽流感 A 病毒及其人畜共患潜力带来的持续挑战。
设计与对象
该研究评估了 Moderna mRNA-1010 疫苗 与标准剂量流感 疫苗 的疗效和安全性,重点关注其减少老年人病例和死亡的潜力。
核心方法
该研究将Moderna的mRNA-1010 疫苗与标准剂量流感疫苗进行了比较,强调了其在快速生产和及时疫苗菌株选择方面的优势。
主要结果
Moderna 的 mRNA-1010 已表现出优于标准剂量流感 疫苗 的功效,同时保持了可接受的安全性。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,mRNA 疫苗 可能会显着改善流感控制和应对未来大流行威胁的准备。
科研与公卫意义
这些发展可能会带来更好的 监测、诊断 以及针对 禽流感 和其他呼吸道病毒的预防策略。
局限与证据强度
证据仅基于摘要数据,该研究未提供详细的统计分析或长期安全性数据。

Are we finally getting on top of influenza?

Authors: Gary S. Grohmann
Original abstract (English)
Seasonal influenza remains a major cause of morbidity and mortality, particularly in older adults, but with United States Food and Drug Administration approval of mRNA vaccines for seasonal influenza for those over 50 years of age, we should see cases and deaths significantly reduced. Moderna’s mRNA-1010 has demonstrated superior efficacy to a standard-dose influenza vaccine while maintaining an acceptable safety profile. The platform offers important advantages, including rapid manufacture and more timely vaccine strain selection, which will result in improved protection against antigenic drift. mRNA technology may also enable combination vaccines targeting influenza, COVID-19 and other respiratory viruses. Together with emerging H5N1 mRNA vaccines, these developments will likely substantially improve influenza control and strengthen preparedness for future pandemic threats.

Question and background
The study addresses the need to improve influenza control and preparedness for future pandemic threats, particularly in light of the ongoing challenges posed by avian influenza A viruses and their zoonotic potential.
Design and population
The study evaluates the efficacy and safety of Moderna’s mRNA-1010 vaccine in comparison to standard-dose influenza vaccines, focusing on its potential to reduce cases and deaths in older adults.
Core methods
The study compares Moderna’s mRNA-1010 vaccine with a standard-dose influenza vaccine, highlighting its advantages in rapid manufacture and timely vaccine strain selection.
Main results
Moderna’s mRNA-1010 has demonstrated superior efficacy to a standard-dose influenza vaccine while maintaining an acceptable safety profile.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that mRNA vaccines may offer significant improvements in influenza control and preparedness for future pandemic threats.
Scientific and public-health significance
These developments could lead to better surveillance, diagnosis, and prevention strategies for avian influenza and other respiratory viruses.
Limitations and evidence strength

疫苗 为下一次新出现的传染病危机做好准备

Vaccine preparedness for the next emerging infectious disease crisis
作者： Florian Krammer, Krammer F.
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了加强 疫苗 针对新出现的传染病的准备的需要,强调了从 SARS-CoV-2 大流行中汲取的经验教训,以及针对未来威胁开发有效的 疫苗 所面临的持续挑战。
设计与对象
该研究是对当前 疫苗 准备策略的评论分析,重点关注粘膜 疫苗 的开发和对新出现病原体的广泛保护性 疫苗,重点是 禽流感 A 病毒。
核心方法
这些方法涉及对现有 疫苗 技术、监管框架和基础研究的最新进展的回顾,包括 ReVAMPP 等项目在促进对新兴病毒的理解中的作用。
主要结果
结果表明,虽然基于 mRNA 的 疫苗 在大流行期间提供更快的发育,但粘膜 疫苗 的准备仍然存在显着差距。此外,研究指出,疫苗 等许可的监管途径仍不清楚。

科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,投资粘膜 疫苗 平台和广泛保护的 疫苗 可以增强对未来 暴发 的准备,特别是涉及 禽流感 A 病毒的准备。
局限与证据强度
该证据基于评论,不包括直接的实验结果,这限制了其强度。由于依赖现有文献和政策讨论,对结论的信心程度较低。

Vaccine preparedness for the next emerging infectious disease crisis

Authors: Florian Krammer, Krammer F.
Original abstract (English)
nature immunology Volume 27 | December 2026 | 2357–2359 | 2357 Vaccine preparedness for the next emerging infectious disease crisis Florian Krammer Recent outbreaks with viruses such as Bundi­bugyo virus and Andes virus are a reminder that another pandemic can happen at any time. This Comment summarizes where we stand with pandemic preparedness from a vaccine perspective. During the COVID-19 pandemic, we witnessed vaccine develop­ment at its finest. Vaccines against the novel coronavirus SARS-CoV-2 were developed at record speed and were made available within approximately 1 year after the outbreak started1. This swift devel­ opment of vaccines was facilitated by new technologies such as the mRNA vaccine platform2 but also by a wealth of exist­ ing knowledge because of many years of virology, vacci­nology and structural biology research. Much has happened since the COVID-19 pandemic. Technology has evolved, new insights into mechanisms of protection and basic immunology have been gained and regulatory and policy views have changed (Fig. 1), especially in the USA. Where does this leave us with vaccine preparedness for emerging pathogens? Mucosal immunity matters A clear lesson learned from the COVID-19 pandemic was that intramus­ cular vaccination can only imperfectly protect from infection with respiratory viruses per se and from onward transmission1. Vaccines that are administered via mucosal surfaces may be a better option to induce immunity in the upper respiratory tract to protect from infection with respiratory viruses. This was clearly identified as a gap, and funding agencies, including the US National Institute of Allergy and Infectious Diseases (NIAID) and Biomedical Advanced Research and Development Authority (BARDA), set out to support the development of muc...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the need for improved vaccine preparedness against emerging infectious diseases, highlighting the lessons learned from the SARS-CoV-2 pandemic and the ongoing challenges in developing effective vaccines for future threats.
Design and population
The study is a commentary analyzing current vaccine preparedness strategies, focusing on the development of mucosal vaccines and broadly protective vaccines for emerging pathogens, with an emphasis on avian influenza A viruses.
Core methods
The methods involve a review of existing vaccine technologies, regulatory frameworks, and recent developments in basic research, including the role of programs like ReVAMPP in advancing understanding of emerging viruses.
Main results
The results indicate that while mRNA-based vaccines offer faster development during pandemics, there remains a significant gap in mucosal vaccine preparedness. Additionally, the study notes that the regulatory pathway for licensing such vaccines is still unclear.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that the current focus on strain-matched vaccines, rather than broadly protective ones, may limit long-term pandemic preparedness. They highlight the novelty of considering mucosal immunity as a key factor in vaccine development.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that investing in mucosal vaccine platforms and broadly protective vaccines could enhance preparedness for future outbreaks, particularly those involving avian influenza A viruses.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is based on a commentary and does not include direct experimental results, which limits its strength. The confidence in the conclusions is moderate due to the reliance on existing literature and policy discussions.

紧急卫生系统指导的特点:使用 AGREE-HS 工具进行评估

Characteristics of emergency health systems guidance: an evaluation using AGREE-HS tool
作者： Weixuan Bai, Danping Zheng, An Li, Gezhi Zhang, Mengyu Liu
摘要中文翻译目的 基于指南研究与评估-卫生系统评估(AGREE-HS)工具,对世界卫生组织(WHO)制定的部分应急卫生系统指南(HSG)文件进行示范性评价,总结和探讨其发展特点。方法 我们检索了 WHO IRIS 数据库(自建库至 2024 年 5 月 20 日),查找有关 COVID-19、EVD、MERS、H1N1、H7N9 和 SARS 的文档。筛选后,三对六名经过培训的评估员表现出一致性(所有 ICC (2,1) >0.75)。所包含的文件使用 AGREE-HS 五个项目(35 个标准)进行评分,并对疾病之间的总体评分和特定项目评分进行比较。结果 纳入了 136 例紧急 HSG(COVID-19101、EVD 13、H1N1 9、MERS 6、SARS 7;没有 H7N9)。三个评估对之间的试点 ICC 分别为 0.824(95% CI:0.343-0.978)、0.814(95% CI:0.395-0.976)和 0.828(95% CI:0.448-0.978),表明一致性良好。总分范围为 31.67%–85.00%(平均 59.07 ± 11.76%)。平均项目得分:主题 5.22、建议 5.15、可实施性 4.32、方法 4.24、参与者 3.79。COVID-19 总体得分最高 (61.16%),高于 EVD(52.44%,p 建议 > 可实施性 > 方法 > 参与者,其中 MERS 和 SARS 的参与者和可实施性最低。Bonferroni 校正后,仅建议存在显着差异(SARS 与 H1N1,p < 0.05;EVD 与 COVID-19,p < 0.05;SARS 与 COVID-19,p < 0.001)和可实施性(SARS 与 COVID-19,p < 0.05);评估者指出,在主题重点和建议清晰度方面存在显着差异,但在竞争利益管理、证据审查透明度和实施策略方面存在缺陷。评估、多学科协作和资源协调,建立高效、可持续的响应系统。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在使用 AGREE-HS 工具评估紧急卫生系统指南 (HSG) 文件,总结和探索其发展特征,满足了解如何创建和应用此类指南以应对不同疾病的需求。
设计与对象
该研究涉及在 WHO IRIS 数据库中搜索有关特定疾病的文档,包括 禽流感,并包含 136 个紧急 HSG,其中与 COVID-19 相关的数量最多 (101),没有与 H7N9 相关的文件。
核心方法
使用 AGREE-HS 五个项目(35 个标准)对文档进行评分,并对疾病之间的总体评分和特定项目评分进行比较。三对六名训练有素的评估员表现出与超过 0.75 的 ICC (2,1) 值的一致性。
主要结果
纳入文献的总分范围为31.67%~85.00%,平均值为59.07±11.76%。平均项目得分为主题 5.22、建议 5.15、可实施性 4.32、方法 4.24 和参与者 3.79。
解释与创新
作者解读说,应急HSG优先考虑针对性议题和务实建议,在确保可实施性的同时优化资源配置和研究方法,这是对此类指南特点的新颖见解。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可为 禽流感 制定更有效和可实施的紧急卫生系统指南提供信息,强调主题重点和建议清晰度在公共卫生应对措施中的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究的证据强度中等,因为它依赖于仅抽象的评估,并且没有提供详细的方法或结果,尽管评估者之间的一致性表明评分可靠。

Characteristics of emergency health systems guidance: an evaluation using AGREE-HS tool

Authors: Weixuan Bai, Danping Zheng, An Li, Gezhi Zhang, Mengyu Liu
Original abstract (English)
Objective Based on the Appraisal of Guidelines Research & Evaluation – Health Systems (AGREE-HS) tool, this study aimed to conduct a demonstrative evaluation of selected emergency health systems guidance (HSG) documents developed by the World Health Organization (WHO), in order to summarize and explore their development characteristics. Methods We searched the WHO IRIS database (inception to May 20, 2024) for documents on COVID-19, EVD, MERS, H1N1, H7N9, and SARS. After screening, six trained appraisers in three pairs showed consistency (all ICC (2,1) >0.75). Included documents were scored with the AGREE-HS five items (35 criteria), and overall and item-specific scores were compared among diseases. Results 136 emergency HSGs were included (COVID-19101, EVD 13, H1N1 9, MERS 6, SARS 7; none for H7N9). The pilot ICCs among the three appraisal pairs were 0.824 (95% CI: 0.343–0.978), 0.814 (95% CI: 0.395–0.976), and 0.828 (95% CI: 0.448–0.978), indicating good agreement. Overall scores ranged from 31.67%–85.00% (mean 59.07 ± 11.76%). Mean item scores: Topic 5.22, Recommendations 5.15, Implementability 4.32, Methods 4.24, Participants 3.79. COVID-19 scored highest overall (61.16%), higher than EVD (52.44%, p Recommendations > Implementability > Methods > Participants, with MERS and SARS lowest in Participants and Implementability. After Bonferroni correction, significant differences remained only for Recommendations (SARS vs. H1N1, p < 0.05; EVD vs. COVID-19, p < 0.05; SARS vs. COVID-19, p < 0.001) and Implementability (SARS vs. COVID-19, p < 0.05); no other item showed significant between-group differences. Appraisers noted strengths in topic focus and recommendation clarity but weaknesses in competing interest management, evidence review transparency, and implementation strategies. Conclusion Emergency HSG prioritizes targeted topic and practical recommendations, optimizing resource allocation and research methods while ensuring implementability. Although description of competing interests and funding management is simplified, these documents demonstrate disciplinary breadth and transparency. High-quality emergency guidance requires balancing rigorous evidence assessment, multidisciplinary collaboration, and resource coordination to build an efficient, sustainable response system.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to evaluate emergency health systems guidance (HSG) documents using the AGREE-HS tool to summarize and explore their development characteristics, addressing the need for understanding how such guidance is created and applied in response to different diseases.
Design and population
The study involved searching the WHO IRIS database for documents on specific diseases, including avian influenza, and included 136 emergency HSGs, with the highest number related to COVID-19 (101) and none for H7N9.
Core methods
Documents were scored using the AGREE-HS five items (35 criteria), and overall and item-specific scores were compared among diseases. Six trained appraisers in three pairs showed consistency with ICC (2,1) values exceeding 0.75.
Main results
The overall scores of the included documents ranged from 31.67% to 85.00%, with a mean of 59.07 ± 11.76%. The mean item scores were Topic 5.22, Recommendations 5.15, Implementability 4.32, Methods 4.24, and Participants 3.79.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that emergency HSGs prioritize targeted topics and practical recommendations, optimizing resource allocation and research methods while ensuring implementability, which represents a novel insight into the characteristics of such guidance.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the development of more effective and implementable emergency health systems guidance for avian influenza, emphasizing the importance of topic focus and recommendation clarity in public health responses.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's evidence strength is moderate, as it relies on an abstract-only evaluation and does not provide detailed methods or results, though the consistency among appraisers suggests reliable scoring.

用光镊量化病毒感染细胞的粘弹性变化。

Quantifying Viscoelastic Alterations in Virus-Infected Cells by Optical Tweezers.
作者： Da-Di Xu, Yawen Zheng, Liu Liu, Yu-Yao Li, Pengfei Xu, Yang Liu, Dai-Wen Pang, Hong-Wu Tang
摘要中文翻译细胞的粘弹性与其生理功能密切相关,影响分化、增殖和迁移等过程。病毒通过感染 宿主 细胞进行复制,并可以影响细胞的粘弹性。然而,目前的研究主要集中在整体细胞粘弹性的变化,而细胞内粘弹性特性仍然难以测量。光镊由于其非接触性质,为测量细胞内粘弹性提供了一种有前途的工具。在本研究中,使用主动-被动校准方法和微流变技术测量了感染三种不同病毒(H9N2、PRV和HSV)的宿主细胞的粘弹性模量。系统分析了不同频率范围内粘弹性模量的变化。结果表明,病毒感染改变了细胞的粘弹性特性,改变的程度因病毒类型而异。我们得出的结论是,病毒感染导致宿主细胞的粘性模量增加和弹性模量降低,这归因于病毒诱导的细胞骨架解聚,从而降低了细胞弹性并增加了粘度。这些发现为病毒引起的细胞粘弹性改变的机制提供了见解,并为进一步研究病毒感染的细胞机制提供了理论基础。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在研究病毒感染如何影响宿主细胞的粘弹性特性,因为这些特性与分化、增殖和迁移等生理功能密切相关。
设计与对象
该研究使用主动-被动校准和微流变技术测量了感染三种不同病毒(H9N2、PRV 和 HSV)的 宿主 细胞的粘弹性模量。
核心方法
使用主动-被动校准方法和微流变技术测量了感染三种不同病毒的宿主细胞的粘弹性模量。
主要结果
结果表明,病毒感染改变了细胞的粘弹性特性,改变的程度因病毒类型而异。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为病毒感染导致 宿主 细胞粘性模量增加和弹性模量降低的证据,这归因于病毒诱导的细胞骨架解聚。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为病毒引起的细胞粘弹性改变的机制提供了见解,并为进一步研究病毒感染的细胞机制提供了理论基础。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告局限性或证据强度。

Quantifying Viscoelastic Alterations in Virus-Infected Cells by Optical Tweezers.

Authors: Da-Di Xu, Yawen Zheng, Liu Liu, Yu-Yao Li, Pengfei Xu, Yang Liu, Dai-Wen Pang, Hong-Wu Tang
Original abstract (English)
The viscoelasticity of cells is closely related to their physiological functions, influencing processes such as differentiation, proliferation, and migration. Viruses replicate by infecting host cells and can affect the cells' viscoelastic properties. However, current research mainly focuses on changes in the overall cellular viscoelasticity, while intracellular viscoelastic properties remain difficult to measure. Optical tweezers, due to their noncontact nature, offer a promising tool for measuring intracellular viscoelasticity. In this study, the viscoelastic moduli of host cells infected with three different viruses (H9N2, PRV, and HSV) were measured using the active-passive calibration method and microrheological techniques. The changes in viscoelastic moduli across different frequency ranges were systematically analyzed. The results showed that viral infection altered the viscoelastic properties of cells, with the extent of changes varying by virus type. We conclude that viral infection leads to an increase in viscous modulus and a decrease in elastic modulus of the host cell, and this is attributed to virus-induced depolymerization of the cytoskeleton, which reduces cellular elasticity and increases viscosity. These findings provide insights into the mechanisms of virus-induced alterations in cellular viscoelasticity and offer a theoretical foundation for further studies on the cellular mechanisms of viral infections.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate how viral infections affect the viscoelastic properties of host cells, as these properties are closely linked to physiological functions such as differentiation, proliferation, and migration.
Design and population
The study measured the viscoelastic moduli of host cells infected with three different viruses (H9N2, PRV, and HSV) using active-passive calibration and microrheological techniques.
Core methods
The viscoelastic moduli of host cells infected with three different viruses were measured using the active-passive calibration method and microrheological techniques.
Main results
The results showed that viral infection altered the viscoelastic properties of cells, with the extent of changes varying by virus type.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as evidence that viral infection leads to an increase in viscous modulus and a decrease in elastic modulus of the host cell, attributed to virus-induced depolymerization of the cytoskeleton.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings provide insights into the mechanisms of virus-induced alterations in cellular viscoelasticity and offer a theoretical foundation for further studies on the cellular mechanisms of viral infections.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations or evidence strength.

2026 年之后的 One Health 优先事项:将科学、监测 和治理方面的进步转化为预防和准备。

One Health priorities beyond 2026: translating advances in science, surveillance and governance into prevention and preparedness.
作者： Tieble Traore, Seif S Al-Abri, Francine Ntoumi, Timothy D McHugh, Giuseppe Ippolito, Rumina Hasan, Avinash Sharma, Eleni Aklillu, Carsten Richter, John Tembo
摘要中文翻译《柳叶刀 2023 年同一个健康》系列强调了人类、动物和环境健康的相互依存性以及多部门承诺与有效实施之间持续存在的差距。从那时起,基因组和环境监测、人工智能和国际治理的进步为传染病预防和准备创造了新的机遇。然而,禽流感 A(H5N1)、丝状病毒 暴发、抗菌素耐药性和 2026 年安第斯病毒游轮 暴发 说明了 监测、协调和响应方面持续存在的挑战。气候敏感感染、食源性人畜共患病和环境污染进一步强调需要采取超越新出现的病毒威胁的综合方法。这篇综述批判性地审视了 2023 年以来的进展,将已建立的预防性干预措施与有前途但未完全验证的技术区分开来,并将近期生物安全控制和结构性社会生态上游预防与早期检测和 Z​​XQTERMXQXZ 遏制区分开来。我们提出了一个六阶段的 监测 行动路径,将检测、验证、联合风险评估、负责任的决策、协调干预和双环评估联系起来。操作矩阵说明了其在新发和地方性感染、抗菌素耐药性和环境威胁方面的应用。五个优先事项涉及可互操作的参与性 监测、基于证据的上游预防、综合抗菌素耐药性行动、纠正部门权力不对称的可持续国家治理以及公平的区域能力。有效的“同一个健康”实施需要明确的机构责任、持续的融资、共同设计的社区和土著参与以及严格的评估。最终应通过传染病风险的明显降低以及对人类、动物和环境健康的保护的改善来衡量进展。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该审查重点关注 禽流感 A 病毒、禽源人畜共患感染和合格的禽类亚型。它解决了 监测、协调和应对这些病原体的挑战,特别是在人类和动物健康相互依赖的背景下。
证据基础与方法
此次审查是对 2023 年以来发展的严格审查,将现有的预防干预措施与有前途但未完全验证的技术区分开来。它概述了一个六阶段的 监测 行动路径,并提出了五个研究和实践优先事项。
共识与结论
该审查强调需要采取综合方法来应对传染病,强调明确的机构责任、持续融资、共同设计的社区参与和严格评估的重要性。它强调了 监测、协调和应对 禽流感 及相关人畜共患感染方面持续存在的挑战。
争议与缺口
审查确定了执行多部门承诺方面的差距以及加强部门间协调的必要性。报告还指出,在降低传染病风险和改善人类、动物和环境健康保护方面缺乏进展。
科研与实践启示
该审查提出了研究和实践的五个优先事项,包括可互操作的参与性监测、基于证据的上游预防、综合抗菌素耐药性行动、可持续的国家治理和公平的区域能力。它还表明需要一个六阶段的 监测 行动途径来改善预防和准备。

One Health priorities beyond 2026: translating advances in science, surveillance and governance into prevention and preparedness.

Authors: Tieble Traore, Seif S Al-Abri, Francine Ntoumi, Timothy D McHugh, Giuseppe Ippolito, Rumina Hasan, Avinash Sharma, Eleni Aklillu, Carsten Richter, John Tembo
Original abstract (English)
The interdependence of human, animal and environmental health and persistent gaps between multisectoral commitments and effective implementation were highlighted in The Lancet 2023 One Health series. Since then, advances in genomic and environmental surveillance, artificial intelligence and international governance have created new opportunities for infectious disease prevention and preparedness. However, avian influenza A(H5N1), filovirus outbreaks, antimicrobial resistance and the 2026 Andes virus cruise-ship outbreak illustrate continuing challenges in surveillance, coordination and response. Climate-sensitive infections, foodborne zoonoses and environmental contamination further emphasize the need for integrated approaches extending beyond emerging viral threats. This review critically examines developments since 2023, distinguishing established preventive interventions from promising but incompletely validated technologies, and both proximate biosecurity controls and structural socio-ecological upstream prevention, from early detection and outbreak containment. We propose a six-stage surveillance-to-action pathway linking detection, verification, joint risk assessment, accountable decisions, coordinated interventions and double-loop evaluation. An operational matrix illustrates its application across emerging and endemic infections, antimicrobial resistance and environmental threats. Five priorities address interoperable participatory surveillance, evidence-based upstream prevention, integrated antimicrobial resistance action, sustainable national governance that redresses sectoral power asymmetries, and equitable regional capacity. Effective One Health implementation requires clear institutional responsibilities, sustained financing, co-designed community and indigenous participation and rigorous evaluation. Progress should ultimately be measured by demonstrable reductions in infectious disease risks and improved protection of human, animal and environmental health.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on avian influenza A viruses, avian-origin zoonotic infections, and qualified avian subtypes. It addresses the challenges in surveillance, coordination, and response to these pathogens, particularly in the context of human and animal health interdependence.
Evidence base and method
The review is a critical examination of developments since 2023, distinguishing established preventive interventions from promising but incompletely validated technologies. It outlines a six-stage surveillance-to-action pathway and proposes five priorities for research and practice.
Consensus and conclusions
The review highlights the need for integrated approaches to address infectious diseases, emphasizing the importance of clear institutional responsibilities, sustained financing, co-designed community participation, and rigorous evaluation. It underscores the persistent challenges in surveillance, coordination, and response to avian influenza and related zoonotic infections.
Controversies and gaps
The review identifies gaps in the implementation of multisectoral commitments and the need for better coordination between sectors. It also notes the lack of progress in reducing infectious disease risks and improving protection of human, animal, and environmental health.
Research and practice implications
The review proposes five priorities for research and practice, including interoperable participatory surveillance, evidence-based upstream prevention, integrated antimicrobial resistance action, sustainable national governance, and equitable regional capacity. It also suggests the need for a six-stage surveillance-to-action pathway to improve prevention and preparedness.

孟加拉国尼帕病毒和 禽流感 病毒的公开基因组证据:对人-动物界面元数据完整性和 监测 代表性的范围评估

Publicly Available Genomic Evidence for Nipah and Avian Influenza Viruses in Bangladesh: A Scoping Assessment of Metadata Completeness and Surveillance Representativeness at the Human–Animal Interface
作者： Md. Nazmul Hossen, Md. Shamim Uddin, Md. Imran Hossain, Tarin Naher, Hossen, M. N., Uddin, M. S., Hossain, M. I.
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
孟加拉国反复出现尼帕病毒 (NiV) 暴发 和持续的 禽流感 病毒 (AIV) 活动。该审查评估了孟加拉国(2001-2025)NiV和AIV基因组记录元数据的完整性,以评估监测的代表性。
证据基础与方法
A structured, source-based scoping assessment of publicly documented NiV and AIV genomic records from Bangladesh (2001–2025), drawing on NCBI GenBank/INSDC and cited peer-reviewed and public-health sources. The pilot registry contained 108 entries (55 NiV, 53 AIV).
共识与结论
8.3% 的条目提供明确的 宿主 信息,7.4% 的条目提供明确的地区信息;这些阻碍了对 宿主、区级和时间代表性的可靠定量评估,并且不能确定 监测 或病毒循环的缺失。登记并不是完整的人口普查。
争议与缺口
由于加入级元数据不可用,无法评估一些分析问题。包含 H5N1 和 H3N8 的 33 个 AIV 组被排除在 H5/H9 特异性解释之外。试点注册表显示出很大的元数据限制。
科研与实践启示
在计算定量代表性指数或地区级优先事项之前,需要使用透明的验证层和统一标识符进行准入级重建。

Publicly Available Genomic Evidence for Nipah and Avian Influenza Viruses in Bangladesh: A Scoping Assessment of Metadata Completeness and Surveillance Representativeness at the Human–Animal Interface

Authors: Md. Nazmul Hossen, Md. Shamim Uddin, Md. Imran Hossain, Tarin Naher, Hossen, M. N., Uddin, M. S., Hossain, M. I.
Original abstract (English)
Bangladesh experiences recurrent Nipah virus (NiV) outbreaks and sustained avian influenza virus (AIV) activity. Whether public genomic records contain metadata needed to assess surveillance representativeness across hosts, locations, and time is unclear.We conducted a structured, source-based scoping assessment of publicly documented NiV and AIV genomic records from Bangladesh (2001–2025), drawing on NCBI GenBank/INSDC and cited peer-reviewed and public-health sources. A pilot registry contained 108 entries (55 NiV, 53 AIV). Metadata were retained only when explicitly reported; unverified fields were recorded as not extracted. The registry is not a complete census. Five analytical questions were prespecified; several could not be evaluated because accession-level metadata were unavailable. The 33-accession AIV set containing H5N1 and H3N8 was excluded from H5/H9-specific interpretation. In the pilot registry, 9/108 entries (8.3%) had explicitly reported host species; the separate host-implied category was not added. Twelve entries (11.1%) had a full collection date; 21 (19.4%) had any collection date. Eight entries (7.4%) had an explicit district. The registry lists 22 NiV accession identifiers from a 2026 study, whereas the publication reports 23 newly sequenced N-gene records; this remains unresolved. Eight AIV accessions ( PV961349.1 – PV961356.1) represent eight segments of one chicken H5N1 isolate, not eight genomes. Twelve MN994-series records are HA-gene sequences from environmental live-bird-market samples.The pilot registry demonstrates substantial metadata limitations. Explicit host information was available for 8.3% of entries and explicit district for 7.4%; these prevent reliable quantitative assessment of host, district-level, and temporal representativeness and do not establish absence of surveillance or viral circulation. Accession-level reconstruction with transparent verification tiers and harmonised identifiers is required before quantitative representativeness indices or district-level priorities can be calculated.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Bangladesh experiences recurrent Nipah virus (NiV) outbreaks and sustained avian influenza virus (AIV) activity. The review assesses the completeness of metadata for NiV and AIV genomic records from Bangladesh (2001–2025) to evaluate surveillance representativeness.
Evidence base and method
A structured, source-based scoping assessment of publicly documented NiV and AIV genomic records from Bangladesh (2001–2025), drawing on NCBI GenBank/INSDC and cited peer-reviewed and public-health sources. The pilot registry contained 108 entries (55 NiV, 53 AIV).
Consensus and conclusions
Explicit host information was available for 8.3% of entries and explicit district for 7.4%; these prevent reliable quantitative assessment of host, district-level, and temporal representativeness and do not establish absence of surveillance or viral circulation. The registry is not a complete census.
Controversies and gaps
Several analytical questions could not be evaluated because accession-level metadata were unavailable. The 33-accession AIV set containing H5N1 and H3N8 was excluded from H5/H9-specific interpretation. The pilot registry demonstrates substantial metadata limitations.
Research and practice implications
Accession-level reconstruction with transparent verification tiers and harmonised identifiers is required before quantitative representativeness indices or district-level priorities can be calculated.

高致病性禽流感 疫苗 对鸡 死亡率 的保护功效:实验挑战试验的荟萃分析和系统评价

Protective Efficacy of Highly Pathogenic Avian Influenza Vaccines Against Mortality in Chickens: A Meta-Analysis and Systematic Review of Experimental Challenge Trials
作者： Fernando Mata, Maria Rosário Marques, João Marques Almeida, Alexandra Eduarda Francisco, Kátia Paulos, Cátia Falcão Martins
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
高致病性禽流感 (HPAI) 仍然是家禽生产和动物福利的主要威胁。
证据基础与方法
这项系统综述和荟萃分析评估了 HPAI 疫苗接种 和 死亡率 在实验挑战鸡中的关联,并探索了异质性的生物学和操作来源。截至 2025 年 8 月 30 日的 Scopus 检索,并辅以参考文献筛选,确定了包含 158 项研究的 66 篇符合条件的文章。使用随机效应模型、调节分析和探索性多变量敏感性分析对 死亡率 相对风险 (RR) 进行分析。使用 SYRCLE 工具评估偏倚风险。
共识与结论
Vaccination markedly reduced mortality (pooled RR = 0.11, 95% CI: 0.08–0.14), although heterogeneity was substantial (I2 = 88.4%). Both recombinant and inactivated vaccines demonstrated protective associations against mortality, but the available evidence did not establish the intrinsic superiority of either platform.
争议与缺口
疫苗 挑战遗传和抗原关系的报告不一致,阻碍了它们作为调节因子的可靠纳入,同时仍然存在大量无法解释的异质性。单变量分析表明 疫苗 平台和给药途径之间存在差异,但探索性调整比较对模型规范和 宿主 免疫、抗原剂量、佐剂配方、病毒进化枝和攻击条件的不完整表征敏感。偏倚风险评估揭示了方法学上的巨大不确定性,而抗原关系报告的不一致阻碍了他们作为调节者的可靠评估。
科研与实践启示
这些发现支持 HPAI 死亡率 作为在实验挑战下减少 死亡率 的一种手段,同时强调需要更好的表征研究来评估抗原匹配、交叉保护和现场条件下的性能。

Protective Efficacy of Highly Pathogenic Avian Influenza Vaccines Against Mortality in Chickens: A Meta-Analysis and Systematic Review of Experimental Challenge Trials

Authors: Fernando Mata, Maria Rosário Marques, João Marques Almeida, Alexandra Eduarda Francisco, Kátia Paulos, Cátia Falcão Martins
Original abstract (English)
Highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) remains a major threat to poultry production and animal welfare. This systematic review and meta-analysis evaluated the association between HPAI vaccination and mortality in experimentally challenged chickens and explored biological and operational sources of heterogeneity. Experimental studies comparing mortality in vaccinated and unvaccinated chickens following HPAI challenge were eligible. A Scopus search up to 30 of August 2025, supplemented by reference screening identified 66 eligible articles comprising 158 studies. Mortality relative risks (RRs) were analysed using random-effects models, with moderator analyses and exploratory multivariable sensitivity analyses. Risk of bias was assessed using the SYRCLE tool. Vaccination markedly reduced mortality (pooled RR = 0.11, 95% CI: 0.08–0.14), although heterogeneity was substantial (I2 = 88.4%). A trim-and-fill sensitivity analysis yielded a less pronounced protective association (RR = 0.335, 95% CI: 0.293–0.383). Univariate analyses suggested differences between vaccine platforms and administration routes, but exploratory adjusted comparisons were sensitive to model specification and incomplete characterisation of host immunity, antigen dose, adjuvant formulation, viral clade and challenge conditions. Inconsistent reporting of vaccine–challenge genetic and antigenic relationships prevented their reliable inclusion as moderators, while substantial unexplained heterogeneity remained. Risk-of-bias assessments revealed substantial methodological uncertainty, while inconsistent reporting of antigenic relationships prevented their reliable evaluation as moderators. Both recombinant and inactivated vaccines demonstrated protective associations against mortality, but the available evidence did not establish the intrinsic superiority of either platform. These findings support HPAI vaccination as a means of reducing mortality under experimental challenge while highlighting the need for better-characterised studies to evaluate antigenic matching, cross-protection and performance under field conditions.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) remains a major threat to poultry production and animal welfare.
Evidence base and method
This systematic review and meta-analysis evaluated the association between HPAI vaccination and mortality in experimentally challenged chickens and explored biological and operational sources of heterogeneity. Consensus and conclusions
Vaccination markedly reduced mortality (pooled RR = 0.11, 95% CI: 0.08–0.14), although heterogeneity was substantial (I2 = 88.4%). Both recombinant and inactivated vaccines demonstrated protective associations against mortality, but the available evidence did not establish the intrinsic superiority of either platform.
Controversies and gaps
Inconsistent reporting of vaccine–challenge genetic and antigenic relationships prevented their reliable inclusion as moderators, while substantial unexplained heterogeneity remained. Research and practice implications

A 高优先级学术文献 · 综述A high priorityAcademic literature · Review

病毒病原体作为全球健康威胁:传播性、致死性和大流行潜力的回顾

Viral Pathogens as Global Health Threats: Review of Transmissibility, Lethality, and Pandemic Potential
作者： Jonathan A. Coffman, Franklin Garcia-Godoy, Peter E. Murray, F. García-Godoy
摘要中文翻译病毒病原体的传播性、致死性以及引起广泛流行病或大流行的可能性差异很大。例如,麻疹具有极强的传染性,但在获得现代医疗护理的人群中病死率相对较低。相反,狂犬病、尼帕病毒、埃博拉病毒和马尔堡病毒可引起严重疾病,病死率高,但通常表现出有限的持续人际传播 传播。因此,最大的全球威胁不一定是最具传染性或致命性的病毒,而是那些结合了有效的 传播、临床严重性、人畜共患 储存宿主、进化适应性和有限的预防或治疗选择的病毒。COVID-19大流行说明了风险因素的这种融合,证明了传播的有效性,包括症状前和无症状传播,再加上临床上重大疾病,可以产生毁灭性的全球后果。甲型流感病毒由于其广泛的动物 储存宿主、抗原 进化 的能力以及人类大流行的历史,仍然是一个主要的大流行问题。尽管尚未确定持续的人传人 高致病性禽流感 A(H5N1),但鉴于在已确诊的人类病例中观察到的高病死率,高致病性禽流感 A(H5N1) 尤其令人担忧。相比之下,亨尼帕病毒和丝状病毒尽管传播能力相对有限,但由于其高致死率而构成重大临床威胁。本综述通过全球健康风险的三个相互关联的决定因素批判性地审查了主要的病毒威胁:传播性、感染死亡风险以及控制其流行和大流行潜力的生物和流行病学因素。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本综述重点关注 禽流感 A 病毒,检查其传播性、感染死亡风险和大流行的可能性。核心问题是这些病毒如何通过其生物学和流行病学特征构成全球健康威胁。
证据基础与方法
该审查是对主要病毒威胁的批判性分析,重点是 禽流感 A 病毒。它没有明确报告数据库、搜索日期或资格标准,但系统地评估了各种病毒实体的传播性、致死性和大流行潜力的相互作用。
共识与结论
禽流感 A 病毒,特别是 H5N1 等高致病性毒株,由于其高病死率、人畜共患 储存宿主 以及进化适应性的潜力,对全球健康构成重大威胁。然而,这些病毒的持续人传人 传播 仍未确定,这凸显了进一步研究的必要性。
争议与缺口
关于 禽流感 A 病毒在人与人之间持续存在的证据有限,特别是对于 H5N1 等高致病性亚型。此外,审查还指出,虽然这些病毒具有很高的致死率,但它们的传播能力通常是有限的,这在了解它们的全部大流行潜力方面造成了差距。
科研与实践启示
需要进一步的研究来更好地了解 禽流感 A 病毒的传播性和进化适应性,特别是在人畜共患 跨物种溢出 事件的背景下。监测 和诊断改进对于有效监控和响应 暴发 也至关重要。

Viral Pathogens as Global Health Threats: Review of Transmissibility, Lethality, and Pandemic Potential

Authors: Jonathan A. Coffman, Franklin Garcia-Godoy, Peter E. Murray, F. García-Godoy
Original abstract (English)
Viral pathogens vary substantially in their transmissibility, lethality, and potential to cause widespread epidemics or pandemics. Measles, for example, is exceptionally contagious but has relatively low case fatality in populations with access to modern medical care. Conversely, rabies, Nipah virus, Ebola virus, and Marburg virus can cause severe disease with high case fatality rates but generally exhibit limited sustained human-to-human transmission. The greatest global threats, therefore, are not necessarily the most contagious or lethal viruses, but those that combine efficient transmission, substantial clinical severity, zoonotic reservoirs, evolutionary adaptability, and limited preventive or therapeutic options. The COVID-19 pandemic illustrated this convergence of risk factors, demonstrating how efficient transmission, including presymptomatic and asymptomatic spread, coupled with clinically significant disease can produce devastating global consequences. Influenza A viruses remain a major pandemic concern because of their extensive animal reservoirs, capacity for antigenic evolution, and established history of human pandemics. Highly pathogenic avian influenza A(H5N1) is particularly concerning given the high case fatality observed among confirmed human cases, although sustained human-to-human transmission has not been established. In contrast, henipaviruses and filoviruses pose substantial clinical threats because of their high lethality, despite comparatively limited transmissibility. This review critically examines major viral threats through three interconnected determinants of global health risk: transmissibility, infection fatality risk, and the biological and epidemiological factors governing their epidemic and pandemic potential.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This review focuses on avian influenza A viruses, examining their transmissibility, infection fatality risk, and pandemic potential. The central question is how these viruses pose global health threats through their biological and epidemiological characteristics.
Evidence base and method
The review is a critical analysis of major viral threats, with an emphasis on avian influenza A viruses. It does not explicitly report on databases, search dates, or eligibility criteria, but it systematically evaluates the interplay of transmissibility, lethality, and pandemic potential across various viral entities.
Consensus and conclusions
Avian influenza A viruses, particularly highly pathogenic strains like H5N1, are significant global health threats due to their high case fatality rates, zoonotic reservoirs, and potential for evolutionary adaptability. However, sustained human-to-human transmission remains unestablished for these viruses, highlighting the need for further research.
Controversies and gaps
There is limited evidence on sustained human-to-human transmission of avian influenza A viruses, particularly for highly pathogenic subtypes like H5N1. Additionally, the review notes that while these viruses have high lethality, their transmissibility is generally limited, creating a gap in understanding their full pandemic potential.
Research and practice implications
Further research is needed to better understand the transmissibility and evolutionary adaptability of avian influenza A viruses, especially in the context of zoonotic spillover events. Surveillance and diagnostic improvements are also critical to monitor and respond to outbreaks effectively.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 综述A high priorityAcademic literature · Review

整合 流行病学 和 宿主 免疫基因组学对抗跨境动物病毒威胁

Integrating Epidemiology and Host Immunogenomics Against Transboundary Animal Viral Threats
作者： Md. Aminul Islam, Muhammad Jasim Uddin, Haruki Kitazawa, Julio Villena
摘要中文翻译跨境动物病毒跨越国界,并越来越多地跨越物种障碍。2019年以来,块状皮肤病病毒在亚洲蔓延并进入西欧,小反刍兽疫病毒进入欧盟,口蹄疫在中欧重新出现,H5N1 禽流感在奶牛中扎根。通常从两个不同的角度研究这些威胁。现场和基因组 流行病学 描述了病毒在哪里传播、如何传播以及它们的成本,而 宿主 免疫基因组学则研究为什么一些动物能够抵抗感染或对 疫苗接种 做出更好的反应。整合这些观点可以更全面地了解 宿主 与病原体的相互作用,并为更有效的疾病预防和控制提供信息。本综述研究了六种代表性病毒:小反刍兽疫病毒(PPRV)、口蹄疫病毒(FMDV)、非洲猪瘟病毒(ASFV)、结节性皮肤病病毒(LSDV)、猪繁殖与呼吸综合征病毒(PRRSV)和H5N1 禽流感病毒,以及它们对动物健康和生产构成的威胁。我们总结了可用于调查这些威胁的流行病学和免疫基因组学方法,并提出了一个将这两个领域与四种控制结果联系起来的综合框架:有针对性的 监测、改进的 疫苗 匹配、抗病选择性育种以及循证决策。我们还评估了该框架在低收入和中等收入国家(LMIC)和小农农业系统中的可行性,这些国家的疾病负担很高,但证据基础仍然有限,并且我们强调,尽管小反刍动物的疾病负担很重,但 宿主 基因组研究集中在猪身上。通过将病毒 传播 的群体水平模式与 宿主 水平的易感性变异和 免疫应答 连接起来,本综述确定了加强 监测 和跨境动物病毒性疾病控制的机会。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该审查重点关注 禽流感 A 病毒,研究它们在跨境动物病毒威胁中的作用,特别是它们对动物健康和生产的影响。它探讨了这些病毒如何跨越国家和物种边界,并研究了 流行病学 和 宿主 免疫基因组学的整合,以更好地了解 宿主 与病原体的相互作用并改善疾病预防和控制。
证据基础与方法
该综述对包括禽流感 A病毒在内的六种代表性病毒进行了系统分析。它概述了用于研究这些病毒的流行病学和免疫基因组学方法,并提出了疾病控制的综合框架。该综述没有明确报告荟萃分析或汇总估计,但强调现有证据的综合。
共识与结论
该审查强调,禽流感 A 病毒构成重大跨境威胁,特别是在它们已在奶牛和其他牲畜中定居的地区。结论是,整合流行病学和免疫基因组数据可以增强对 宿主 病原体动态的理解,并导致更有效的疾病控制策略。
争议与缺口
The review notes that 宿主 genomic research is disproportionately focused on pigs,despite the high disease burden in small ruminants.它还确定了中低收入国家 (LMIC) 和小农农业系统的证据基础差距,这些国家的疾病控制最具挑战性。
科研与实践启示
该综述提出了具体的研究和实践重点,包括有针对性的 监测、改进的 疫苗 匹配、抗病选育以及循证决策。它强调需要对小型反刍动物进行更多的基因组研究,并更好地整合不同 宿主 物种和地区的数据。

Integrating Epidemiology and Host Immunogenomics Against Transboundary Animal Viral Threats

Authors: Md. Aminul Islam, Muhammad Jasim Uddin, Haruki Kitazawa, Julio Villena
Original abstract (English)
Transboundary animal viruses cross national borders and, increasingly, species barriers. Since 2019, lumpy skin disease virus has spread across Asia and into Western Europe, peste des petits ruminants virus has entered the European Union, foot-and-mouth disease has reemerged in Central Europe, and H5N1 avian influenza has become established in dairy cattle. These threats are usually studied from two separate perspectives. Field and genomic epidemiology describe where viruses circulate, how they spread, and what they cost, whereas host immunogenomics investigates why some animals resist infection or respond better to vaccination. Integrating these perspectives can provide a more complete understanding of host–pathogen interactions and inform more effective disease prevention and control. This review examines six representative viruses: peste des petits ruminants virus (PPRV), foot-and-mouth disease virus (FMDV), African swine fever virus (ASFV), lumpy skin disease virus (LSDV), porcine reproductive and respiratory syndrome virus (PRRSV), and H5N1 avian influenza virus, and the threats they pose to animal health and production. We summarize the epidemiological and immunogenomic approaches available to investigate these threats and propose an integrated framework linking both fields to four control outcomes: targeted surveillance, improved vaccine matching, selective breeding for disease resistance, and evidence-informed policymaking. We also assess the feasibility of this framework in low- and middle-income countries (LMICs) and smallholder farming systems, where disease burdens are high but the evidence base remains limited, and we highlight that host genomic research is concentrated in pigs despite the heavy disease burden in small ruminants. By connecting population-level patterns of viral transmission with host-level variation in susceptibility and immune response, this review identifies opportunities to strengthen the surveillance and control of transboundary animal viral diseases.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on avian influenza A viruses, examining their role in transboundary animal viral threats, particularly their impact on animal health and production. It explores how these viruses cross national and species borders and investigates the integration of epidemiology and host immunogenomics to better understand host–pathogen interactions and improve disease prevention and control.
Evidence base and method
The review is a systematic analysis of six representative viruses, including avian influenza A viruses. It outlines the epidemiological and immunogenomic approaches used to study these viruses and proposes an integrated framework for disease control. The review does not explicitly report on meta-analysis or pooled estimates but emphasizes the synthesis of available evidence.
Consensus and conclusions
The review highlights that avian influenza A viruses pose significant transboundary threats, particularly in regions where they have become established in dairy cattle and other livestock. It concludes that integrating epidemiological and immunogenomic data can enhance understanding of host–pathogen dynamics and lead to more effective disease control strategies.
Controversies and gaps
The review notes that host genomic research is disproportionately focused on pigs, despite the high disease burden in small ruminants. It also identifies gaps in the evidence base for low- and middle-income countries (LMICs) and smallholder farming systems, where disease control is most challenging.
Research and practice implications
The review proposes specific research and practice priorities, including targeted surveillance, improved vaccine matching, selective breeding for disease resistance, and evidence-informed policymaking. It emphasizes the need for more genomic research on small ruminants and better integration of data across different host species and regions.

平衡小规模散养鸡系统中的动物福利和 禽流感 生物安全

Balancing Animal Welfare and Avian Influenza Biosecurity in Small-Scale Free-Range Chicken Systems
作者： Qihan Niu
摘要中文翻译小规模的户外养鸡造成了一个棘手的生物安全问题。户外通道支持有价值的畜牧业,但可能使羊群暴露于野生动物和受污染的环境中。这篇结构化的叙述性评论综合了 20 个有目的地选择的来源,基于小型户外鸡群的高致病性禽流感暴露和与福利相关的控制。选定的证据表明,日常饲养和农场联系也会影响暴露程度。直接基于动物的福利证据很少;大多数与福利相关的工作都考察了饲养员的担忧或实际后果。清洁的日常管理可以与有针对性的野生动物措施和 疫苗接种 相结合,尽管它们在小群中的联合表现仍不确定。因为吸收取决于当地条件和实际负担,意识并不能确保持续的实践。因此,生物安全应解决可靠的暴露途径,而不造成可避免的伤害或不切实际的负担。未来的研究应使用标准化福利指标和纵向现场数据来评估联合干预措施是否可以减少高致病性禽流感暴露,同时保持可接受的福利标准。这一证据将支持为小规模户外养鸡者制定适当的、实用的、适合当地的生物安全指南。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述重点关注与 禽流感 A 病毒相关的小规模散养鸡系统的生物安全挑战。核心问题是如何平衡动物福利与有效的生物安全措施,以防止接触 禽流感 A 病毒和其他相关病原体。
证据基础与方法
该评论是对 20 个有意选择的来源的结构化叙述综合,重点是小型户外鸡群中高致病性禽流感的暴露和与福利相关的控制。证据基础包括关于户外活动对 禽流感 A 病毒暴露的影响以及常规畜牧业和农场联系在形成这种暴露中的作用的研究。
共识与结论
证据表明,日常饲养和农场联系显着影响 禽流感 A 病毒的暴露。然而,直接基于动物的福利证据是有限的,大多数研究检查饲养员的担忧或实际后果,而不是直接的福利结果。
争议与缺口
缺乏直接的基于动物的福利证据,大多数研究关注饲养员的担忧,而不是鸡的实际福利。此外,清洁日常管理、有针对性的野生动物措施和 疫苗接种 等干预措施在减少高致病性禽流感暴露同时维持福利标准方面的联合表现仍不确定。
科研与实践启示
未来的研究应使用标准化福利指标和纵向现场数据来评估综合干预措施在减少高致病性禽流感暴露同时维持可接受的福利标准方面的有效性。这一证据将支持为小规模户外养鸡者制定适当的、实用的、适合当地的生物安全指南。

Balancing Animal Welfare and Avian Influenza Biosecurity in Small-Scale Free-Range Chicken Systems

Authors: Qihan Niu
Original abstract (English)
Small-scale outdoor chicken keeping creates a difficult biosecurity problem. Outdoor access supports valued husbandry but can expose flocks to wildlife and contaminated environments. This structured narrative review synthesised 20 purposively selected sources, based on HPAI exposure and welfare-related control in small outdoor flocks. The selected evidence showed that routine husbandry and farm connections also shaped exposure. Direct animal-based welfare evidence was scarce; most welfare-relevant work examined keeper concerns or practical consequences. Cleaner daily management may be combined with targeted wildlife measures and vaccination, although their joint performance in small flocks remains uncertain. Because uptake depends on local conditions and practical burden, awareness does not ensure sustained practice. Biosecurity should therefore address a credible exposure pathway without creating avoidable harm or an impractical burden. Future studies should use standardised welfare indicators and longitudinal field data to evaluate whether combined interventions can reduce HPAI exposure while maintaining acceptable welfare standards. This evidence would support the development of proportionate, practical, and locally adaptable biosecurity guidance for small-scale outdoor chicken keepers.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on the biosecurity challenges in small-scale free-range chicken systems related to avian influenza A viruses. The central question is how to balance animal welfare with effective biosecurity measures to prevent exposure to avian influenza A viruses and other related pathogens.
Evidence base and method
The review is a structured narrative synthesis of 20 purposively selected sources, with a focus on HPAI exposure and welfare-related control in small outdoor flocks. The evidence base includes studies on the impact of outdoor access on avian influenza A virus exposure and the role of routine husbandry and farm connections in shaping this exposure.
Consensus and conclusions
The evidence indicates that routine husbandry and farm connections significantly influence exposure to avian influenza A viruses. However, direct animal-based welfare evidence is limited, with most studies examining keeper concerns or practical consequences rather than direct welfare outcomes.
Controversies and gaps
There is a lack of direct animal-based welfare evidence, and most studies focus on keeper concerns rather than the actual welfare of the chickens. Additionally, the joint performance of interventions such as cleaner daily management, targeted wildlife measures, and vaccination in reducing HPAI exposure while maintaining welfare standards remains uncertain.
Research and practice implications
Future research should use standardised welfare indicators and longitudinal field data to evaluate the effectiveness of combined interventions in reducing HPAI exposure while maintaining acceptable welfare standards. This evidence would support the development of proportionate, practical, and locally adaptable biosecurity guidance for small-scale outdoor chicken keepers.
